# Experiments
Aliases: Chromatron, Chronomatron, Experiment, Superpairs, Ultrasequencer
Categories: Pages with UIs, More info needed
[Experiments]
Experiments are minigames that grant Enchanting XP and Enchanting-related rewards. Experiments are performed on the [[Experimentation Table]].
## Mechanism
A player must first obtain an [[Experimentation Table]] and place it on their [[Private Island]]. Right-Clicking on it opens the Experiments interface.
The [[Experimentation Table]] has three charges, with every Main Experiment costing one charge. One charge gets renewed after 24 hours and players can purchase additional games up to 3 times, each being more expensive than the last.
|Reset|Cost|
|-|-|
|1|50 [[Experience]] Levels 150 [[Bits]]|
|2|100 [[Experience]] Levels 300 [[Bits]]|
|3|200 [[Experience]] Levels 500 [[Bits]]|
The table features two Add-On Experiments and the Main Experiment. The Main Experiment can be done once the player completes both Add-On Experiments.
## Add-On Experiments
Add-Ons Experiments have to be played before the Main Experiment and allow the player to gain extra clicks, as well as some bonus [[Enchanting|Enchanting XP]].
- Add-Ons are memory related mini-games, best compared to a simple sequence memory test and the "Chimpanzee Test".
- Upon finishing an Add-On the player can either accept the rewards or decline them to try again for a better score. Subsequent add-on attempts will however cost [[Experience]].
### Add-On Experiment: Chronomatron
Chronomatron Completion Rewards
A visual and auditory memory game in which the player must memorize a chain of color-coded "notes" and recite them.
- When the player successfully recites the chain, the same chain is played with an additional note added onto the end.
- The game ends when the player misses a note or completes 49 note chains, although the player is rewarded for completing at most 15 note chains. There are various tiers of this experiment:
|Stake|Colors on Board|Rewards Enchanting XP ([[Enchanting]]) per Note in Longest Chain; (→ cap at a Chain of 15)|Rewards Superpairs Clicks| Enchanting Level ([[Enchanting]]) Required (same as above)| [[Experience]] Levels Restart Cost|
|-|-|-|-|-|-|
|High|3|+1,500 (→22,500)|Chain of 5: +1 Chain of 9: +2|Enchanting XX (20) ([[Enchanting]])|5|
|Grand|5|+2,500 (→37,500)|Chain of 5: +1 Chain of 9: +2|Enchanting XXV (25) ([[Enchanting]])|10|
|Supreme|7|+3,500 (→52,500)|Chain of 5: +1 Chain of 9: +2|Enchanting XXX (30) ([[Enchanting]])|15|
|Transcendent|8|+4,500 (→67,500)|Chain of 5: +1 Chain of 9: +2|Enchanting XXXV (35) ([[Enchanting]])|20|
|Metaphysical|10|+6,000 (→90,000)|Chain of 5: +1 Chain of 9: +2 Chain of 12: +3|Enchanting XL (40) ([[Enchanting]])|40|
### Add-On Experiment: Ultrasequencer
A visual memory game in which the player must memorize numeric sequences which appear for a brief amount of time and then recite the sequences by clicking on the squares where they were.
- Each time the player successfully completes a sequence, a new, more difficult sequence with one additional number is displayed.
- The game ends when the player misses a number or finishes the round with all slots filled, though the maximum reward is always reached after completing the 20 slot sequence.
|Stake|Grid Size|Rewards Enchanting XP ([[Enchanting]]) per Number in Longest Sequence; (→ cap at a Sequence of 20)|Rewards Superpairs Clicks| Enchanting Level ([[Enchanting]]) Required (same as above)| [[Experience]] Levels Restart Cost|
|-|-|-|-|-|-|
|Supreme|7x3|3500 (→70,000)|Sequence of 5: +1 Sequence of 7: +2|Enchanting XXV (25) ([[Enchanting]])|15|
|Transcendent|7x4|5000 (→100,000)|Sequence of 5: +1 Sequence of 7: +2|Enchanting XXX (30) ([[Enchanting]])|20|
|Metaphysical|9x4|7000 (→140,000)|Sequence of 5: +1 Sequence of 7: +2 Sequence of 9: +3|Enchanting XL (40) ([[Enchanting]])|40|
## Main Experiment: Superpairs
A memory matching game, where the player reveals two pieces of the board at a time and the goal is to match items in pairs.
- Each tile is labeled with a different reward, and if the player clicks two identical pieces consecutively, they get the reward.
- There is a set amount of time and a set amount of Clicks.
Note: The Possible pairs XP bottles does not represent how many pairs of that specific bottle that the player can get in one experiment but rather how many are given from one pair.
There are Power Ups a player can get while doing Superpairs. These are:
- +1 Click and +x [[Experience]]
- +3 Clicks;
- Instant Find on next Click.

Ultra-Rare Books and [[Endcaps|Endcap Upgrades]] have a chance to be obtained from Supreme Experiments and above and can be increased via following methods:
|Source|Boost|
|-|-|
|MYTHIC ([[Rarity]]) [[Guardian Pet]]|7%|
|Enderman Slayer VIII (8) ([[Enderman Slayer]])|15%|
Base chance from Metaphysical Experiments is 1% (1 in 100 experiments), meaning that individual Ultra-Rare Books and [[Endcaps|Endcap Upgrades]] have a base 0.027% (1 in 3,700) chance to appear.

## Interface
Tip: click on certain slots to change the UI or go to another article!
## Tips
- Experiments are currently the best way to level up [[Enchanting]].
- An [[Rarity|EPIC]] [[Guardian Pet]] grants up to 30 [[Enchanting Wisdom]]
## Trivia
- The player used to be able to receive the +3 click bonus on every difficulty, not just Metaphysical. This bug was patched in 0.19.4.
- The maximum experience reward is given after passing 15 chain of notes on Chronomatron or 20 numbers on Ultrasequencer.
  - This means that even if the player passes more than 15 notes on the Chronomatron, they will receive only a maximum of 90,000 XP at most (on Metaphysical difficulty) and similar with 140,000 XP for the Ultrasequencer.
- It used to be possible to use an insta-find power-up on another power up, which would not trigger the effect, this was later patched now correctly giving 2 instafinds afterwards .
- Bits spent on renewing experiments used to not give the player [[Fame Ranks|Fame]]. This was later patched [More Info Needed] (More infomation needed; When this info is added, remove this.).
- If the server the player is on reboots while the player is playing Chronomatron or Ultrasequencer, they will lose their progress. Players can restart, but the addons will be treated as if the player had never started it. Players are advised to wait for a bit If the servers are rebooting for an update.
- There are several mods that make Chronomatron and Ultrasequencer easier such as Skyblocker and SkyHanni. Note that enabling more than one of these modules at once may cause issues such as double-clicking which would fail the addons.
- The Ultrasequencer will always have each number follow a specific set of colors, allowing it to be solved more easily without the need of mods. The pattern of colors goes from white, light blue, blue, purple, magenta, pink, red, orange, yellow, lime green, green, and cyan. Once the player finishes 12 rounds, the color pattern will repeat for the next 12 and so on until the entire board is solved.
- It used to be possible to get more than one pair of [[Titanic Experience Bottle|Titanic Experience Bottles]] while playing Superpairs, with no visual indication of which items belong to which pair. This forced players to rely on luck when trying to pair these bottles. This was however later patched [More Info Needed]
- It is possible to obtain multiple tiers of the same enchantment in the same Superpairs, for example [[Protection|Protection VI]] and [[Protection|Protection VII]].
- Completing the board on Ultrasequencer will only count as a sequence of 35, meaning it is impossible to obtain a sequence of 36.
- A bug causes one less round to be required for maximum Chronomatron rewards.

# Fairy Souls
Aliases: Fairy Soul, Soul, Souls
Categories: Pages with UIs, Temp/Using Skill Aliases, Mechanics, Fairy Souls, Quest items, Health, Strength, Defense, Speed
[Fairy Souls]
Fairy Souls are collectibles scattered around the SkyBlock world. Collecting them grants [[SkyBlock Levels|SkyBlock XP]] and [[Storage]] [[Backpack]] slots once exchanged at [[Tia the Fairy]].
## Mechanics
Fairy Souls take the form of rainbow-coloured orbs scattered and hidden throughout parts of the SkyBlock world. Players can interact with Fairy Souls to be collected, but each soul can only be collected once. Some Fairy Souls are also obtained through other means, such as [[Fishing]] and [[NPC]] purchases.
There are currently 289 Fairy Souls in total. Fairy Souls may be exchanged in groups of 5 with [[Tia the Fairy]], an [[NPC]] in the [[Wilderness]].
For each exchange, the player will receive [[SkyBlock Levels|10 SkyBlock XP]] and will unlock [[Backpack]] slots at exchange #1, #2, #4, #6, #8, #10, #13, #16, #19, #22, #25, #28, #31, #34, #37, #40, and #44; or in other words, after giving Tia 5, 10, 20, 30, 40, 50, 65, 80, 95, 110, 125, 140. 155, 170, 185, 200, and 220 Fairy Souls respectively.
Players can view a breakdown of how many Fairy Souls they have in total and from which islands in their [[Quests|Quest Log]] in the [[SkyBlock Menu]].
Note that the listed breakdown numbers for Miscellaneous Fairy Souls are totals and do not indicate the player's progress. Additionally, the listed counts for Dungeons and Fishing are wrong – there are 8 Dungeons and 5 Fishing Fairy Souls (see below for details).
## Game Messages
|Condition|Message|
|-|-|
|Finding a new Fairy Soul|SOUL! You found a Fairy Soul!|
|Clicking a Fairy Soul already found before|You have already found that Fairy Soul!|
## List of Fairy Souls
All Fairy Soul locations, except those obtained through fishing and [[The Garden]], can be seen below. In-game, [[Coordinates]] can be viewed by pressing F3 on the keyboard.

- [[Fairy Souls/List/The Farming Islands|The Farming Islands]]

### Tips
- It is recommended to use a mod that highlights Fairy Souls.
  - SkyHanni has waypoints and pathing for Fairy Soul locations, which can be enabled in its config (/sh fast fairy souls).
  - Skyblocker has waypoints for Fairy Soul locations, which can be enabled in its config (/skyblocker config fairy souls helper) -> Enable Fairy Souls Helper.
- Even though some Fairy Souls are stated to be walkable, it is still strongly recommended to use an [[Aspect of the End]] or [[Aspect of the Void|Void]] and [[Ender Pearl|Ender Pearls]]. Early-game players may choose to opt for the [[Grappling Hook]], a cheaper alternative to an AOTE.
### Miscellaneous Souls
There are several Fairy Souls that cannot be obtained through normal means.
#### Dungeons
Up to 8 Fairy Souls can be found in various [[Dungeons|Dungeon]] rooms.
Note: The game incorrectly lists 9, but one of them was removed.
#### Fishing
Five Fairy Souls are obtained from [[Fishing]]. The 2nd, 9th, and 24th treasure catches fished up by the player are guaranteed to be Fairy Souls. Two additional Fairy Souls have a 0.5% chance to be fished up in a [[Fairy Grotto]].
Note: The game incorrectly lists 4 total Fishing Fairy Souls rather than 5.
#### Garden
The first order accepted from [[Tia the Fairy]] when she visits [[The Garden]] grants a single Fairy Soul. Likewise, the first order accepted from [[Bruuh]] when he visits [[The Garden]] grants a Fairy Soul.
#### Placeable
One Fairy Soul can be obtained from the [[Placeable Fairy Soul]], which can be placed on the [[Private Island]] and used by anyone visiting it.
#### Glacite Mineshafts
A Fairy Soul can be found near the Vanguard Corpse in Fairy [[Glacite Mineshafts|Mineshafts]].
## Trivia
- According to [[Tia the Fairy]], Fairy Souls are souls of her dead sisters.
- Fairy Souls are meant to spawn when a Fairy dies, this raises a question regarding some Fairy Souls that are obtained from various puzzles or tasks.
  - Killing a [[Fairy]] in [[Catacombs|The Catacombs]] does not spawn a Fairy Soul.
- The [[Fairy Armor]] plays a distinctive sound when the player is near an uncollected Fairy Soul.
- A player who had already collected seven Fairy Souls from the [[Floating Islands]] prior to the v0.7.4 update will have only four counted souls in the Quest under the section for "The Park".
- Prior to version 0.7.11, players were only able to legitimately obtain 194 Fairy Souls, but a small portion of players were able to obtain an additional six due to a bug resulting from the Park Update for a total of 200. One player who took advantage of this bug was 12[[Ranks|[ADMIN]]] TimeDeo, formerly 7[[Ranks|[YOUTUBE]]], who is frequently associated with Fairy Souls thanks to some of his YouTube videos displaying them.
  - [[Resistance General|TimeDeo]] later became a Minister, granting a bonus to the stats that Fairy Souls used to give, before the 0.15 Patch.
- Due to some Fairy Souls being given by NPCs and the existence of [[Placeable Fairy Soul]], it is implied that they are a physical object.
- As of version 0.27, there are 289 Fairy Souls in the game, and since they are exchanged with [[Tia the Fairy]] in groups of 5, the final 4 Fairy Souls a player collects give no extra reward.

# Fast Travel
Categories: Pages with UIs, Mechanics, SkyBlock Menu
[Fast Travel]
Fast Travel is one of the features in the [[SkyBlock Menu]]. It allows players to instantly teleport to different public islands in the game.

|SkyBlock Hub ([[Hub Island]])|Sirius' Shack ([[Dark Auction]])|/warp da||Travel Scroll ([[Travel Scrolls]]); 5[MVP+] ([[Ranks]])|

# Forest Essence
Aliases: Foraging Essence, Foraging essence
Categories: Pages with UIs
[Forest Essence]
Type: [[Currency]]
[[Forest Essence]] Forest Essence is an [[Essence]]. It is primarily used on [[Galatea]].
## Obtaining
[[Forest Essence]] Forest Essence can be be obtained in [[Galatea]] in multiple ways:
- [[Tree Gifts]]
- [[Starlyn Contest|Starlyn Contests]]
- [[Salvaging]] certain items and items upgraded with [[Forest Essence]] Forest Essence.
### Increasing Forest Essence Gain
There are several sources that can be used to increase Forest Essence gained:
|Icon|Name|Type|Effect|
|-|-|-|-|
|[[Dreadwing Shard]]|Essence of the Forest ([[Attributes]])|Attribute ([[Attributes]])|Gain +1%–13% more [[Forest Essence]] Forest Essence.|
|[[Komodo Dragon Shard]]|Echo of Essence ([[Attributes]])|Attribute ([[Attributes]])|(same as above)|
|[[Tiamat Shard]]|Echo of Echoes ([[Attributes]])|Attribute ([[Attributes]])|(same as above)|
## Usage
### Star Upgrades
[[Forest Essence]] Forest Essence can be used to upgrade certain items through [[Star Upgrades]].
### Essence Shop
[[Forest Essence]] Forest Essence can be used in the Forest Essence Shop by talking to [[Rosemary]] in the [[Murkwater Outpost]].

# Fossil Essence
Aliases: Fossil Dust, Fossil essence
Categories: Pages with UIs
[Fossil Essence]
Type: [[Currency]]
[[Fossil Essence]] Fossil Essence is an [[Essence]]. It is primarily used in the [[Glacite Tunnels]].
## Obtaining
[[Fossil Essence]] Fossil Essence can be be obtained in the [[Glacite Tunnels]] and [[Glacite Mineshafts]] in multiple ways:
- [[Fossil Excavator]]
- Mining Fossils (which appear as [[Nether Quartz|Quartz]] blocks, stairs, and slabs) in [[Glacite Mineshafts]], rewarding 4-8 each.
- Collecting [[Dr. Stone]]'s rewards.
- Collecting [[Fossil Muncher]]'s rewards.
- [[Salvaging]] certain items.
### Increasing Fossil Essence Gain
[[Gemstone|Peridot Gemstones]] can be applied to a [[Chisel]] to increase [[Fossil Essence]] Fossil Essence gain from the [[Fossil Excavator]] by 30%-100% per gemstone, up to 300% with 3 [[Gemstone|Perfect Peridot Gemstones]] on a [[Perfect Chisel]].
## Usage
### Essence Shop
[[Fossil Essence]] Fossil Essence can be used in the Fossil Essence Shop by talking to [[Researcher Beryl]] in the [[Fossil Research Center]].

# Fossil Excavator
Aliases: Excavating, Excavator, Fossil Excavation
Categories: Pages with UIs, Templates with deprecated argument names
[Fossil Excavator]
The Fossil Excavator is a feature in the [[Dwarven Base Camp]]'s sub area [[Fossil Research Center]] at [[Coordinates|19, 120, 227]] that allows players to use [[Chisels]] and [[Suspicious Scrap]] in order to get rewards.
## Mechanics
### Chisels
A [[Chisels|Chisel]] needs to be selected in order to undergo the Excavating process, all Chisels function the same, but the upgraded Chisels have more Gemstone Slots which provide various bonuses when excavating and also have higher number of Charges. Chisel is not consumed or damaged by the Excavating process.
#### Chisel Gemstone Slots
Applying [[Gemstone|Gemstones]] to [[Chisels]] will give different perks. All chisels except for the the basic [[Chisel]] have [[Gemstone Slot|Chisel Gemstone Slots]]. Each Gemstone type grants a different buff to the Chisel, and is not affected by rarity, unlike all other instances of Gemstone being applied:
|Gemstone Slot|Gemstone Name|Bonus|Value by gemstone tier Rough|Value by gemstone tier Flawed|Value by gemstone tier Fine|Value by gemstone tier Flawless|Value by gemstone tier Perfect|
|-|-|-|-|-|-|-|-|
|[[Gemstone]]|Aquamarine|#% chance to gain an additional Chisel ([[Chisels]]) charge upon starting the Fossil Excavator.|30%|40%|50%|60%|100%|
|[[Gemstone]]|Citrine|#% chance to highlight a piece of treasure upon starting the Fossil Excavator.|30%|40%|50%|60%|100%|
|[[Gemstone]]|Onyx|#% chance to spawn an additional piece of treasure upon starting the Fossil Excavator.|30%|40%|50%|60%|100%|
|[[Gemstone]]|Peridot|Gain +#% Fossil Essence when using the the Fossil Excavator.|30%|40%|50%|60%|100%|
### Suspicious Scrap
The second required item for Excavating is a [[Suspicious Scrap]], which can be obtained by mining in [[Glacite Tunnels]], looting [[Frozen Corpses]], or purchasing for 100 (1 stack plus 36) [[Fossil Essence]] at the [[Essence Shops|Fossil Essence Shop]]. The Suspicious Scrap is consumed upon Excavating.
## Drops
### Fossils
Main reward from Excavating are [[Fossils]], most of them can be obtained from excavating, except [[Helix Fossil]] and [[Claw Fossil]]. Fossils can be donated to [[Dr. Stone]] to get various rewards. Fossils have a 10% chance to appear, all 6 of them are equally rare.
### Other Drops
The amount of non-fossil drops from Excavation is random with various chances listed below. The amount of non-fossil drops can be increased by applying [[Gemstone|Onyx]] to the selected [[Chisels|Chisel]] before beginning Excavation.
|Amount|Chance|
|-|-|
|10|30%|
|11|25%|
|12|20%|
|13|15%|
|14|10%|
|Item|Weight|Chance per roll|Probability per excavation (11.5 rolls)|
|-|-|-|-|
|20 Flawed Aquamarine Gemstone ([[Gemstone]])|450|6.95% (1 in 14.39 rolls)|56.32% (1 in 1.78 excavations)|
|40 Flawed Aquamarine Gemstone ([[Gemstone]])|300|4.63% (1 in 21.58 rolls)|42.05% (1 in 2.38 excavations)|
|1 Fine Aquamarine Gemstone ([[Gemstone]])|150|2.32% (1 in 43.17 rolls)|23.63% (1 in 4.23 excavations)|
|2 Fine Aquamarine Gemstone ([[Gemstone]])|75|1.16% (1 in 86.33 rolls)|12.54% (1 in 7.97 excavations)|
|20 Flawed Peridot Gemstone ([[Gemstone]])|450|6.95% (1 in 14.39 rolls)|56.32% (1 in 1.78 excavations)|

Each roll has a 0.0002% (1 in 500k) chance to be replaced with a [[Fossil Dye]].
## Tips
- To be guaranteed to find fossils using a solver mod (assuming no misclicks), at least 18 charges ( [[Reinforced Chisel]]) are required. Certain solvers may require 20 charges ( [[Glacite-Plated Chisel]]) for some of the fossils.
- A [[Perfect Chisel]] with 3 [[Gemstone|Perfect Citrine Gemstone]] will provide the most treasure per excavation on average.
- It is recommended to do fossil excavation during a [[2x Powder]] event.

# Furniture Shop
Aliases: Carpenter Shop, Carpenter's House
Categories: Pages with UIs, Shops, Furniture
The Furniture Shop has many different kinds of cool furniture for you to put in your world!
— [[Apprentice]]
The Furniture Shop, also known as Carpenter Shop, is a shop located in the basement of the [[Carpenter]]'s house. Various types of [[Furniture]] can be bought there. All of the items sold at the Furniture Shop can also be crafted with the [[Carpentry Table]].
## Shop
|Icon|Item|Cost ([[Coins]])|Notes|
|-|-|-|-|
|[[Furniture]]|Scarecrow|120||
|[[Furniture]]|Weapon Rack|100|Showcase / Storage - This weapon rack lets you show off 4 weapons (swords, pickaxes, or axes)|
|[[Furniture]]|Armor Stand|100|Showcase / Storage - This armor showcase lets you show off a full set of armor, plus a single weapon (sword, pickaxe, or axe)|
|[[Furniture]]|Flower Pot|150||
|[[Furniture]]|Enchanting Table+|10,000|Replicates Enchanting Table functions, comes with the power of a maxed-out Enchanting Table|
|[[Furniture]]|Crafting Table+|10,000|Replicate Crafting Table Functions|
|[[Furniture]]|Furnace+|15,000|Replicates Furnace functions|
|[[Furniture]]|Wood Chest+|15,000|Extra-large chest with 3 tabs|
|[[Furniture]]|Fireplace|10,000|Replicates furnace functions|
|[[Furniture]]|Chest Storage|15,000|Extra large chest with three tabs|

This is an example of the item selling interface in the Furniture Shop.

# Garden Chips
Aliases: Chip, Chips, Cropshot, Cropshot Chip, Evergreen, Evergreen Chip, Garden Chip, Hypercharge Chip, Mechamind Chip, Overdrive Chip, Quickdraw Chip, Rarefinder, Rarefinder Chip, Rarefinder chip, Sowledge Chip, Synthesis Chip, Vermin Vaporizer Chip
Categories: Pages with UIs
[Garden Chips]
Garden Chips are items unlocked from various areas regarding [[The Garden]] and [[Farming]] that provide permanent buffs. They can be upgraded with ✿ [[Sowdust]], which increases their bonuses up to Level 20. Garden Chips can increase their rarity by consuming extras of the same type.
The Garden Chips interface can be accessed by talking to [[Jeff]], in the [[SkyBlock Menu]] while in [[The Garden]], in the [[Farming]] Skill menu, or via the /chips command.
## List of Garden Chips
|Slot|Name|Ability (RARE ([[Rarity]])/EPIC ([[Rarity]])/LEGENDARY ([[Rarity]]))|Obtaining|
|-|-|-|-|
|[[Garden Chips]]|Vermin Vaporizer Chip|Vermin Vaporizer; Grants +3/+4/+5 [[Bonus Pest Chance]] per chip level (up to +30/+60/+100 ).|Rarely dropped by [[Dragonfly]] (2%)|
|[[Garden Chips]]|Synthesis Chip|Synthesis; Analyzing a mutation in the Crop Analyzer gives 1%/1.5%/2% more [[Copper]] per chip level (up to 10%/22.5%/40%).|Rarely dropped from [[Greenhouse]] Harvest Bounty (3%)|
|[[Garden Chips]]|Sowledge Chip|Sowledge; Grants +1/+1.25/+1.5 [[Farming Wisdom]] per chip level (up to +10/+18.75/+30 ).|Purchased from SkyMart ([[The Garden]]) for 200 [[Copper]]|
|[[Garden Chips]]|Mechamind Chip|Mechamind; Grants 1.5%/2%/2.5% more Farming Tool experience per chip level (up to 15%/30%/50%).|Purchased from [[Anita]] for 1 Gold Medal ([[Medals]])|
|[[Garden Chips]]|Hypercharge Chip|Hypercharge; Temporary [[Farming Fortune]] buffs are 3%/4%/5% stronger per chip level (up to 30%/60%/100%).|Rarely obtained from completing Visitor Offers ([[The Garden]]) (2%)|
|[[Garden Chips]]|Evergreen Chip|Evergreen; Gain 2%/2.5%/3% more base crops when harvesting in the [[Greenhouse]] (up to 20%/37.5%/60%).|Rarely dropped from [[Greenhouse]] Harvest Bounty (3%)|
|[[Garden Chips]]|Overdrive Chip|Overdrive; Grants +5/+6/+7 [[Crop Fortune]] for the active crop during [[Jacob's Farming Contest]] per chip level (up to +50/+90/+140 ).|Purchased from [[Anita]] for 2 Gold Medal ([[Medals]])|
|[[Garden Chips]]|Cropshot Chip|Cropshot; Grants +3/+4/+5 [[Farming Fortune]] per chip level (up to +30/+60/+100 ).|Purchased from SkyMart ([[The Garden]]) for 500 [[Copper]] 1 given for free by [[Jeff]]|
|[[Garden Chips]]|Quickdraw Chip|Quickdraw; Decreases the time for Visitors ([[The Garden]]) to appear when harvesting crops by 1.5%/2%/2.5% per chip level (up to 15%/30%/50%).|Rarely obtained from completing Visitor Offers ([[The Garden]]) (2%)|
|[[Garden Chips]]|Rarefinder Chip|Rarefinder; Grants +1.5/+2/+2.5 [[Overbloom]] per chip level (up to +15/+30/+50).|Rarely drops from farming crops at a base chance of 0.0005% (1 in 200,000)|
## Upgrading
### Rarity
Garden Chips can be consumed by right-clicking them. Additionally, using shift right-click consumes all Garden Chips held.
Consuming a Garden Chip for the first time unlocks its effect,and consuming more upgrades its rarity. The amount of Garden Chips required to upgrade its rarity is as follows:
|Rarity|Chips Required|Total Chips Required|
|-|-|-|
|RARE ([[Rarity]])|1|1|
|EPIC ([[Rarity]])|4|5|
|LEGENDARY ([[Rarity]])|16|21|
### Leveling
✿ [[Sowdust]] is used to upgrade a Garden Chip's level. The max level of a Garden Chip increases with rarity.

# Healer
Categories: Pages with UIs, Dungeons, Classes
The Healer Dungeon Orb
Healer is a dungeon [[Classes|class]] that specializes in healing and support.
## Class Passives
- Renew: Your healing abilities heal the target for 50% additional HP.
- Healing Aura: Passively heals all living teammates within a 8 block radius for 0.5% [[Health]] per second
- Revive: Spawns a fairy that follows you and uses its energy to revive dead teammates, while no-one is dead, it will stick and provide support. Vanishes for 100 seconds after reviving a player.
- Orbies: Enemies that you help kill have a 15% chance to drop an orb that anyone can pick up. The orb heals the player who picked it up and grants them a random buff.
  - There are 3 types of orbs and each can be picked up by any teammate for quick heal for 30% of their maximum health, as well as a short [[Ability Damage]], [[Damage]] or [[Defense]] buff.
- Soul Tether: Clicking on a teammate creates a tether connecting yourself to them. Tethered teammates received 0.5% of your maximum health as healing every second while they are within 30 blocks of you. Every 5 melee hits you deal while tethered will heal both players for 1% of the maximum health.
- Overheal: Healing a player that is full health will convert the healing into an absorption shield up 20% to of their health.
## Dungeon Orb Abilities
- Healing Circle: Launches a healing orb that creates a healing circle where it lands. The healing circle heals friendly players inside for 2% of their health every second. Cooldown: 2 seconds
- Wish: Heals everyone in your group to full health and grants them an [[Absorption]] shields for 20% of their max health. Cooldown: 120 seconds
## Ghost Abilities
- Healing Potion: Throw a potion which heals all teammates in a 10 block radius for 10% of their max health and +100 [[Health]]
- Revive: Revive yourself after 50 seconds.
## Leveling Rewards
You can level up the healer class while playing as a healer or if you have a teammate that is Healer, you get some of its healer XP as a team bonus.
For every healer level up to level 50 you get:
- + 1% Renew Healing
- + 2 Ghost Healing Potion Healing
- + 1% Wish Shield Percent
- - 1s Revive Cooldown
- + 0.4% Overheal Maximum Shield
- +0.2 [[Vitality]]
- +0.5 [[Mending]]
For every other healer level you get:
- + 1% Ghost Healing Potion Healing
For every 5 healer levels you get:
- + 0.1% Healing Aura Healing
- + 0.5% Healing Circle Healing
- + 0.5 Healing Circle Radius
- + 1 Healing Circle Duration
At level 50 you get the following buffs:
- Abilites heal for +50% additional hp, for a total of +100%
- Healing Aura heals for +1% more, for a total of 2% of [[Health]]
- -50s revive cooldown, lowering the cooldown to 50s
- +50% Wish Shield, for a total of an [[Absorption]] shield 70% of their maximum health
- Ghost Healing Potion Heals for 100+ and +25% more, for a total of 200 [[Health]] and 35% of their max health
- Healing Circle heals for +5%, for a total of 7% of HP per second
- Overheal gives a shield for up to 40%
- +20.2 [[Vitality]]
- +25.5 [[Mending]]
## Milestones
Healer class can reach its milestones by healing players in the dungeon. Refer [[Class Milestones|here]] for a table showcasing class milestones.
## Trivia
- The Orbies perk resembles the set bonus of the Nebula Armor from Terraria, which also causes orbs to drop and provides the same bonuses of health, defense and ability damage.
- Healer's ultimate might be a reference to Soraka's ultimate from League of Legends or a high-level mage spell in Dungeons & Dragons as it all function similarly and have the same name.
- The Soul Tether ability may be a reference to Io's Tether ability in Dota 2 as they share the concept of extra restoration within a certain distance.

# Legacy Trades
Categories: Pages with UIs, Mechanics, SkyBlock Menu
Legacy Trades is a SkyBlock Mechanic where players can trade legacy items in their inventory for new items. Its menu can be accessed by /ltrades or /legacytrades [[Commands|Command]].
## Game Message
If any Legacy Items that can be exchanged in Legacy Trades are in the player's Inventory or Sacks, the following message will appear:
You currently have one or more Legacy Items in your inventory or sacks that are no longer used throughout the game! Exchange them in the Legacy Trades menu, accessed through /legacytrades!

# Mage
Aliases: Mage Beam, Mage Class, Mage beam, Mage class
Categories: Pages with UIs, Dungeons, Classes
The Mage Dungeon Orb
Mage is a dungeon [[Classes|class]] that specializes in dealing high Magic and area of effect damage. Most Magic Weapons have strong area of effect abilities that can clear out huge waves of mobs but in return struggle with tankier mobs such as mini bosses.
## Class Passives
- Mage Staff: All your melee weapon attacks become ranged. Those attacks deal (30%+0.9×Intelligence1000) of their melee [[Damage Calculation|damage]] up to 10 blocks, after which damage is reduced.
- Efficient Spells: All abilities have a 25% shorter cooldown.
## Dungeon Orb Abilities
- Guided Sheep: Shoots a Guided Sheep, dealing damage based on your Mage level! Can destroy weak walls and crypts inside a dungeon. (Cooldown: 30s)
- Thunderstorm: Unleash a Thunderstorm, striking enemies in a 10 block radius for 15 seconds dealing damage based on your Mage level!
## Ghost Abilities
- Instant Wall: Create a 5x3 wall at the block you are looking at which lasts for 10 seconds
- Fireball: Shoots a fireball, dealing damage based on your Mage level!
## Leveling Rewards
You can level up the Mage class while playing as a Mage or if you have a teammate that is a Mage, you get some of its Mage XP as a team bonus.
For every Mage level up to level 50 you get:
- + 5 [[Intelligence]]
- + 100-2000 Guided Sheep Damage
- + 80-1600 Fireball Damage
For every other Mage level you also get:
- + 1% Cooldown Reduction
Every 5 Mage levels grants:
- +1 [[Ability Damage]]
At level 50 you get the following buffs:
- +250 [[Intelligence]], for a total of +500 [[Intelligence]]
- +10 [[Ability Damage]], for a total of +15 [[Ability Damage]]
- +25% Cooldown Reduction, for a total of -50%
## Strategies
Mage tactics can be categorized into the two following meta:
Left Click Mage: utilizes the mage beam (the mage staff passive) to do extremely high amounts of [[Damage]]. This is an alternative to the Berserker class, as while you can achieve much higher numbers, mage beam is less consistent than [[Berserk|berserker]] attacks, and you also get less [[Effective Health]]. The best Left Click Mage armors have both high [[Intelligence]], [[Strength]], and [[Crit Damage]] (for example, [[Precursor Gear|Ancient]] [[Storm's Armor]] is very good for left click mage). The [[Dark Claymore]] and [[Midas' Sword]] with the ultimate enchantment [[Chimera]] are currently the best weapons for this play style. This is because they both have very high base damage, which when paired with the right armor, equipment and pet allows players to achieve insane amounts of damage. It is recommended to optimize for [[Strength]] and [[Crit Damage]] when getting stone powers and stats tuning for your [[Accessories]]. Good pets for this play style include damage pets such as the [[Ender Dragon Pet]] and [[Golden Dragon Pet]].
Right Click Mage: Utilizes weapon abilities to deal less damage than mage beam, better suited against clusters of mobs. The best armors are either armor sets that provide good [[Effective Health]], like [[Necromancer Lord Armor]], or armor sets that provide a lot of [[Mana]], like [[Storm's Armor]] or [[Wise Dragon Armor]]. Some weapons that can be used include [[Dreadlord Sword]] (early game), [[Bonzo's Staff]] (early-mid game), [[Frozen Scythe]] (early-mid game), [[Spirit Sceptre]] (mid game), [[Yeti Sword]] (mid game), [[Midas Staff|Midas' Staff]] (late game), [[Ice Spray Wand]] (more used for its ability which freezes all enemies as opposed to its [[Ability Damage]], late-end game), and [[Hyperion]] (or Astraea for more ehp, late-end game). As for pets, both the [[Sheep Pet]] and [[Golden Dragon Pet]] increase output damage significantly with the Sheep also reducing mana consumption. It is still often recommended to use both left and right click for this strategy, as melee hits in dungeons regenerate 5 + 1% of max mana. Also, with the mana steal enchant from Enderman Slayer, melee hits can regenerate up to 5 + 1.6% of max mana. When tuning accessories, it is best to use the Inspired Power at first, but as soon as you can buy Ender Monocles for the Sighted power, buy it. The ability damage and intelligence are superior to all powers save Bizarre, which is late game due to its absurd cost.
For more information visit Tutorials/Mage Guide.
## Milestones
Mage class can reach its milestones by dealing magic damage in the dungeon. Refer [[Class Milestones|here]] for a table showcasing class milestones.

# Minions
Aliases: Farming Minion, Farming Minions, Farming minion, Farming minions, Mining Minion, Mining minion, Minion, Minion Modifiers, Minion Slots, Minion Speed
Categories: Pages with UIs, Minions, Minion Modifiers, Mechanics
[Minions]
Minions are item generators constantly active on the [[Private Island]] regardless of whether or not the island is loaded.
## Overview
Minions are item generators that are constantly active on the [[Private Island]]. They interact with the 5x5 area around them by placing and breaking [[Blocks]], or spawning and killing [[Mob|Mobs]]. By default, only 5 of them can be placed at a time, but this limit can be increased by gaining Minion Slots. Most minions also give [[Skills|Skill XP]]. The tier of a minion determines its base storage capacity and speed, both of which can be increased using Minion Modifiers.
The [[Private Island]] starts with a Tier I [[Cobblestone Minion]], which starts with 20 [[Cobblestone]].
Minions cannot be sold on the [[Auction House]], but they can be traded through [[Trading]].
### Minion Interface
The left of the Minion Interface consists of 1 [[Minion Skins|Minion Skin]] slot, 1 [[Minion Fuel]] slot, 1 [[Automated Shipping]] slot, 2 [[Minion Upgrades|Minion Upgrade]] slots, and up to 15 Storage slots.
- The Redstone Torch icon can be clicked to view the minion’s most efficient layout.
- The Minion Head icon can be used to view general information about the minion, including the time between its actions, its max storage, and its items generated.
- The Gold Ingot icon can be used to view how the minion would change if it was upgraded, and can be clicked to view the crafting recipe for the next tier minion.
- The Chest icon can be clicked to pick up all items stored inside the minion.
- The Diamond icon can be used to upgrade the minion's tier (provided all the crafting materials are in the Inventory.
- The Bedrock icon can be used to pick up the minion, converting it to item form, and picking up all upgrades and generated items stored inside.
### Minion Collection Experience
When collecting items from Minions or their storage, the [[Co-op]] will be rewarded with [[Skills|Skill Experience]]. Skill Experience from minions is affected by [[Wisdom]]. If the Co-op’s minions are collected while offline, XP will be received the next time the [[Private Island]] is visited.
## Minion Slots
Minion Slots refer to the number of minions that can have placed on the [[Private Island]] at the same time, and are shared between the [[Co-op]]. By default, this number is 5, but it can be increased up to 32 by crafting more unique tiers of minions and purchasing [[Account & Profile Upgrades|Minion Slots]] from [[Elizabeth]].
Each minion has multiple tiers, and crafting each tier at least once contributes to the amount of slots gained:
|Required Crafts|Bonus Slots|
|-|-|
|5|1|
|15|2|
|30|3|
|50|4|
|75|5|
|100|6|
|125|7|
|150|8|
|175|9|
|200|10|
|225|11|
|250|12|
|275|13|
|300|14|
|350|15|
|400|16|
|450|17|
|500|18|
|550|19|
|600|20|
|650|21|
|700|22|
### Crafted Minions
The Crafted Minions menu can be accessed through the [[Collections|Collection Menu]], /craftedgenerators, or /craftedminions. It displays all the unique minions the [[Co-op]] have crafted. The Minion Head icon at the top displays how many unique minions have been crafted, how many minion slots have been unlocked, and how many unique minions needed until the next minion slot can be unlocked. With each new type of minion unlocked, one of the gray dye icons will be replaced with an icon of that minion type, with a list of each tier unlocked listed in green and each tier yet to be unlocked listed in red.
Additionally, crafting unique minions also grants [[SkyBlock Levels|SkyBlock XP]]:
|Tier|SkyBlock XP|
|-|-|
|I-VI|1|
|VII|2|
|VIII|3|
|IX|4|
|X|6|
|XI|12|
|XII|24|

## Minion Modifiers
### Minion Upgrades
Minion Upgrades items are placed in minions on the Yellow Stained Glass Pane icon. Two Minion Upgrades per minion can be used at a time, and they usually affect how minions behave in regard to range, speed, and items produced.
### Minion Fuel
Minion Fuel items are placed in minions on the Orange Stained Glass Pane icon to upgrade their speed or amount of items generated. Some of them are permanent, and some of them are temporary and run out. While only one type of fuel can be used at once, temporary fuel sources can be stacked on top of each other. They can be retrieved and replaced at any time, provided that for temporary fuel, their individual timer has not started ticking down.
### Minion Storage
Minion Storage comes in the form of a chest that can be placed next to minions for more item storage. Only 1 Minion Storage can be used per minion. The excess storage can be accessed by right-clicking on the Minion Chest, or by clicking 'Collect All' while inside the Minion GUI.
### Automated Shipping
Automated Shipping items are placed in minions on the Blue Stained Glass Pane icon. Automated Shipping is used to sell excess items after minions have run out of storage. The prices for the items depend on the NPC sell price of the item and the type of Automated Shipping used. Clicking on an Automated Shipping in a minion with [[Coins]] stored in it will put the [[Coins]] in the [[Purse]]. Clicking again will take the item out of the minion.

### Minion Skins & Minion Chair
Minion Skins override a specific minion's appearance, but do not affect performance. The Minion Chair is a [[Furniture]] item that minions can sit on. Sitting minions do not work or take up Minion Slots. A minion skin cannot be applied to a sitting minion.
## Mechanics
### Time Between Actions
Minions with a description that includes Time Between Actions will generate collectable items every other action. For example, if a Tier I [[Cobblestone Minion]] has a Time Between Actions of 14 seconds, it will generate 1 [[Cobblestone]] every 28 seconds, not 14 seconds.
#### Time Between Actions Calculation
Minion speed boosts are additive. The minion's Time Between Actions are decreased according to the following formula:
Time Between Actions=Base Time Between Actions1+Combined Speed Boost Percentage
For a Tier XI [[Clay Minion]] with [[Enchanted Coal]], the Base Time Between Actions is 16 seconds, and the speed increase from the [[Enchanted Coal]] is +10%, so the new Time Between Actions would be as follows:
Time Between Actions=16seconds100%+10%≈14.55seconds
### Mob Spawning
A two-dimensional representation of where a mob can spawn around a minion (top block height is based on the height of mob).
The mechanics of [[Mob]] Spawning minions are similar to that of Monster Spawners in vanilla Minecraft:
- The "Ideal Layout" shows the general spawning area for mobs around a minion.
- Minions can spawn mobs only within a 5x5 area centered around them, unless they have a [[Minion Expander]].
- The vertical range in which they spawn is 6 blocks (3 below, 2 above, and 1 at eye level with the minion).
- As long as there is a block for them to spawn on and spawning will not cause them to take suffocation damage, they will spawn in accordance with the minion's normal spawning times.
- Mobs that are wider than 1 block ( [[Slime (disambiguation)|Slimes]], [[Magma Cube|Magma Cubes]], [[Ghast|Ghasts]], etc.) are limited by the horizontal range, but they will still spawn partially inside nearby walls and may possibly escape.
- If a mob is outside of a minion's range, it will not kill it automatically and collect the loot.
## Minion Dialogue
All minions can say and ask questions to each other or the player. These dialogues appear on top of a minion when the minion speaks.

|Player attempting to upgrade the tier of a minion without having the recipe unlocked With Quick Upgrade button|You can't craft that item.|
|(same as above) With Supercrafting|You haven't unlocked this recipe yet!|
|Player attempting to click the Next Tier button without having the recipe unlocked|You haven't unlocked that recipe!|
## Tips
- SkyBlock Minions Spreadsheet - Extensive spreadsheet detailing everything about individual Minion profits from the [[Bazaar]] and [[NPC|NPCs]].
- A Minion with a [[Super Compactor 3000]] or [[Dwarven Super Compactor]] will turn items into their enchanted forms, even if the player has not unlocked the enchanted recipe of the item yet.
## Trivia
- Minion tiers can be distinguished by a colored patch on their heads.
Visual comparison of Minion head patch colors.
|Tier|Head Patch Color|
|-|-|
|I|No Patch|
|II-IV|Light Green|
|V-VII|Purple|
|VIII-X|Red and Gold|
|XI|Diamond and Gold|
|XII|Diamond and Obsidian|
- Increasing a Minion's level sometimes upgrades its held item.
- Minions used to have colored names.
- If a Minion does the same action twice, it slightly decreases the efficiency of the minion.
- In [[The Catacombs - Floor IV]] and above, the [[Catacombs Puzzle Rooms|Quiz]] puzzle has a question about the amount of unique minions.
- The Pickup Minion button used to be able to make bugged-stacks of items like a stack of 64 (1 stack) [[Ender Pearl]].
- On April Fool's Day, Minions appear as regular-sized [[Vanilla Objects|Armor Stands]].
- Mobs killed by Minions drop their base drops as item entities, the minion picks them up after a few seconds. In that short time that the item entities exist, the player or a hopper can pick them up, causing the minion to not get them.
- There used to be a bug where putting a Minion in a [[Chest]] had a small chance of turning it into a Steve or Alex head.
- There used to be a bug where Minion heads could be placed directly on [[Hard Glass]].

- Coal Minion IV
- Cobblestone Minion I Menu
- Cobblestone Minion Layout (White Glass represents air)
- Quartz Minion Upgrade Interface

# Party Finder
Categories: Pages with UIs, More info needed, Temp/Using Skill Aliases, Catacombs, Dungeons, Kuudra
[Party Finder]
Party Finder is a system that allows players to join and set up parties for the [[Catacombs]] and [[Kuudra]]. They are accessed from [[Mort]] and [[Crag]] respectively.
## Overview
Party Finder allows players to set up parties for grouped content, such as the [[Catacombs]] and [[Kuudra]].
Players can start a new queue, allowing them to set up their desired Catacombs floor or Kuudra tier, a group note, and any other requirements.
Tip: click on certain slots to change the UI or go to another article!
Tip: click on certain slots to change the UI or go to another article!
|Icon|Option|Description|
|-|-|-|
|Refresh|Refresh|Refreshes the list of active groups matching your search. Has a cooldown of [More Info Needed] (More infomation needed; When this info is added, remove this.).|
|Search Settings|Search Settings|Allows the player to change their settings to find groups suited to them.|
|[[Block of Redstone]]|Start A New Queue|Allows the player to set up their own Party, with its own personalized settings and group note.|
### Catacombs
The Catacombs Party Finder has the following Search Settings options:
- Select Floor: Allows the player to choose the desired floor of the [[Catacombs]] they want to attempt.
- Select Dungeon Type: Allows the player to choose between attempting the Catacombs or Master Mode.
- Set Class Level: Sets the [[Classes|Class]] level required to be able to join a party. For example, a player at [[Archer|Archer 21]] would not be able to join a party with a Class requirement of 25.
- Set Dungeon Level: Ditto, but sets the desired [[Catacombs]] level instead.
- Enter Search: Allows the player to search for groups containing certain keywords.
- Sort Groups: Allows the player to sort groups between Recently Created, Highest Combat Level, and Largest Group Size.
The Catacombs Party Finder also has the following Group Builder options:
- Dungeon: Allows the party leader to choose between the Catacombs or Master Mode.
- Floor: Allows the party leader to select their desired Dungeon floor.
- Search Text: Allows the party leader to set a custom group note.
- Set Class Level: Sets the [[Classes|Class]] level required to be able to join a party. For example, a player at [[Archer|Archer 21]] would not be able to join a party with a Class requirement of 25.
- Set Dungeon Level: Ditto, but sets the [[Catacombs]] level required to be able to join a party instead.
### Kuudra
The Kuudra Party Finder has the following Search Settings options:
- Select Kuudra Tier: Allows the player to choose the desired tier of [[Kuudra]] they want to attempt, between any tier, Basic, Hot, Burning, Fiery, and Infernal.
- Set Combat Level: Sets the [[Combat]] level required to be able to join a party. For example, a player at [[Combat|Combat XLVIII (48)]] would not be able to join a party with a Combat requirement of [[Combat|Combat L (50)]].
- Enter Search: Allows the player to search for groups containing certain keywords.
- Sort Groups: Allows the player to sort groups between Recently Created, Highest Combat Level, and Largest Group Size.
The Kuudra Party Finder also has the following Group Builder options:
- Select Tier: Allows the party leader to choose the desired tier of [[Kuudra]] they want to attempt, between any tier, Basic, Hot, Burning, Fiery, and Infernal.
- Set Group Note: Allows the party leader to set a custom group note.
- Set Combat Level: Allows the party leader to set the [[Combat]] level required to be able to join a party.
## History
[[SkyBlock Prototype]]:
|Jul 7, 2020|0.8|Added Party Finder.|
|Apr 20, 2022|0.13|Added Party Finder to Kuudra.|
Linked from: [[Changelog]], [[Pale Citadel]]

# Pelts
Aliases: Pelt
Categories: Pages with UIs, Currencies, Quest Rewards
[Pelts]
Type: [[Currency]]
Pelts are a [[Currency]] used to purchase various items from [[Tony|Tony's Shop]] and [[Talbot]].
## Obtaining
Pelts can be obtained from two different NPCs,
### Trevor
Pelts can be obtained by completing [[Trevor]]'s tasks. The amount of Pelts received depends on the mob's rarity and any modifiers.
Modifiers:
|Modifier|Increase|
|-|-|
|[[Pelt Belt]]|+1|
|[[Jake's Plushie]]|+1|
|[[Trapper Crest]]|1.1×–1.5×|
|Rarity|Pelts Default|Pelts Max|
|-|-|-|
|Trackable|2|6|
|Untrackable|4|9|
|Undetected|6|12|
|Endangered|8|15|
|Elusive|16|27|
[[Trevor]] also rewards 2 Pelts as a [[The Garden|Garden]] visitor.
### Shepherd
Players can also obtain 4 Pelts by completing the [[Shepherd]]'s quest.
## Usage
Pelts can be used as a currency in two shops, [[Tony]]'s, which sells T12 Farming minions, and [[Talbot]]'s which sells various farming items.

# Personal Bank
Aliases: Personal Bank Upgrade
Categories: Pages with UIs, Upgrades, Emerald Collection, SkyBlock Menu
[Personal Bank]
Type: Remote Bank Access
The Personal Bank is an Upgrade that allows the player to access the [[Bank]] remotely.
## Interface
Tip: click on certain slots to change the UI or go to another article!
## Usage
The Personal Bank can be accessed through the [[SkyBlock Menu]] after reaching [[Emerald|Emerald VI (6)]]. It allows the player to access the [[Bank]]'s interface from anywhere in [[SkyBlock]]. The Personal Bank has a default cooldown of 1 hour, and during this time cannot be accessed. The cooldown can be reset by talking to the [[Banker]], [[Banker Broadjaw]] or [[Banker Barry]] and can be reduced by talking to [[Guy]].
If the player has a [[Booster Cookie]] active, the Personal Bank can also be accessed through the /bank command.
[[Bingo|Ⓑ Bingo]] players automatically have the Personal Bank unlocked with no cooldown.
### Upgrading
The Personal Bank's cooldown can be reduced by talking to [[Guy]].
|Cooldown|Cost|Emerald Collection|
|-|-|-|
|20 minutes|200,000 [[Coins]]|Emerald VIII (8) ([[Emerald]])|
|5 minutes|1,000,000 [[Coins]]|Emerald IX (9) ([[Emerald]])|
|No cooldown|5,000,000 [[Coins]]|250,000 (3,906 stacks plus 16) Emeralds ([[Emerald]])|
## Trivia
- Using the Personal Vault menu option will not trigger the cooldown. However, if money transfers were done, the Personal Vault cannot be accessed either before the cooldown expires.
## History
[[SkyBlock Prototype]]:
|Jun 11, 2019|0.1|Added Personal Bank.|
|Jun 26, 2019|0.4|Personal Bank will now count down rather than counting up.|
|Dec 3, 2019|Personal Bank can now be upgraded by [[Guy]].|
|Dec 4, 2019|Changed Personal Bank cooldowns to 1h, 20m, 5m, none (was: 2h, 1h, 20m, none).|
|Feb 21, 2020|0.7.6|Personal Bank now only goes on cooldown when it is actually used.|
|Dec 12, 2023|0.19.9|Added /bank command.|

# Pets
Aliases: Alchemy Pet, Alchemy Pets, Alchemy pet, Alchemy pets, Combat Pet, Combat Pets, Combat pet, Exp Sharing, Farming Pet, Farming Pets, Farming pet, Fishing Pet, Fishing Pets, Fishing pet, Fishing pets, Foraging Pet, Foraging Pets, Foraging pet, Hunting Pet, Hunting pet, Mining Mount, Mining Pet, Mining Pets, Mining pet, Mining pets, Mythic Pets, Pet, Pet Exp, Pet Level, Pet Leveling, Pet Score, Pet Xp, Pet level, Pet leveling, Pet xp
Categories: Pages with UIs, Confirmations needed, Pets, Traveling Zoo, Common items, Uncommon items, Rare items, Epic items, Mythic items, Legendary items, Combat, Mining, Foraging, Fishing, Farming, Enchanting, Alchemy, Taming, Hunting
[Pets]
Pets are companions that can be summoned, giving bonus [[Stats]] and one or more different perks. They can be accessed and managed in the Pets Menu, which can be accessed through the [[SkyBlock Menu]].
The stats and perks that a Pet gives are dependent on its level and rarity. Most pets have a specific [[Skills|Skill]] attributed to them. Pets can level up by gaining Skill XP, and they gain the most Skill XP when the player gains XP for its attributed Skill (i.e. a [[Zombie Pet]] would gain the most XP from [[Combat]], a [[Rabbit Pet]] would gain the most XP from [[Farming]], etc.). Most pets belong to one of 9 skills: [[Combat]], [[Mining]], [[Fishing]], [[Foraging]], [[Farming]], [[Enchanting]], [[Alchemy]], [[Taming]], and [[Hunting]]. The [[Bingo Pet]] and [[Precursor Drone Pet]] are All Skills pets. The [[Wisp Pet]] is a Gabagool pet, and the [[Montezuma Pet]] is a Fractured Soul pet. Gabagool Pets are leveled up by being fed [[Gabagool (disambiguation)|Gabagool]].
Pets can be obtained in multiple ways:
- Crafting, through the use of an [[Egg]], [[Enchanted Egg]], [[Super Enchanted Egg]], or [[Omega Enchanted Egg]]
- Mob drops, from mobs like [[Ender Dragon|Ender Dragons]], [[Scatha|Scathas]], [[Yeti|Yetis]], or [[The Loch Emperor|Loch Emperors]], which are affected by [[Magic Find]] and [[Pet Luck]]
- Purchasing, from places like the [[Dark Auction]] or specific merchants;
- Forging, through [[The Forge]];
- Milestone rewards, such as [[Mining]], [[Fishing]], and the [[Kuudra]] boss collection.
Most pets that are crafted start at [[Rarity|COMMON]], and must be upgraded through [[Kat]]; however, some pets only start at certain rarities, like [[Rarity|RARE]] or [[Rarity|EPIC]]. Some pets can be upgraded to [[Rarity|MYTHIC]] at [[Kat]], and will need a special item to upgrade.
## Pets Menu
The Pets Menu can be opened by using the [[Commands]] /viewpetsmenu, /petsmenu, /petmenu, /pets (empty by default), or by accessing it from the [[SkyBlock Menu]]. Here, players can view and manage their pets, view their Pet Score and Autopet rules, and manage more options.
Pets Menu can hold a maximum of 112 pets by default. It can be upgraded with 5,000 [[Bits]] to unlock 28 more slots. This upgrade can be bought twice.
Tip: click on certain slots to change the UI or go to another article! You may have visited: [[SkyBlock Menu/UI]]
### Exp Sharing
Exp Sharing is a system that allows players to level up pets without having them deployed. Players can assign a Pet to an Exp Share slot, upon which the Pet will gain the summoned Pet's XP at a reduced rate.
- Players have 1 slot unlocked by default, while the other 2 slots are only functional while [[Diana]] is in office with her Sharing is Caring perk active.
- The player can increase the percent shared with the following factors, stacking additively
|Exp Shared|Type of source|Source|Condition|
|-|-|-|-|
|0.2% → 12%|Skill ([[Skills]])|[[Taming]] level (+0.2% per level)||
|1% → 10%|Attribute ([[Attributes]])|Why Not More ([[Attributes]]) ( [[Toucan Shard]])||
|15%|Pet Item ([[Pet Items]])|[[Exp Share]]|Pet is in an active Exp Share slot|
|10%|Mayor ([[Mayor Election]]) perk|[[Diana]]'s Sharing is Caring perk||
|Max. = 47%||||
- Pets in Exp Share slots do not gain Pet XP if a pet is not summoned.
- Pets in Exp Share slots that are actively deployed do not gain increased Pet XP.
- Pet XP gained this way is still subject to the multiplicative factors.
### Pet Score
Pet Score is a metric that rewards [[Magic Find]] and [[SkyBlock Levels|SkyBlock XP]] as the player collects unique pets of higher rarities and levels.
- Pet Score:
  - Unique pets award +1→6 Pet Score based on their rarity. If the player possesses duplicates of the same pet, only the one with the highest rarity, or the highest level if tied, is considered.
  - [[Rarity|SPECIAL]] and [[Rarity|VERY SPECIAL]] pets do not count toward Pet Score.
  - Pets at their maximum level award +1 Pet Score.
- [[SkyBlock Levels|3 SkyBlock XP]] are granted for each point of Pet Score.
- [[Magic Find]] is granted at certain thresholds of Pet Score.
Pet Rarity conversion:
|Pet Rarity|Pet Score|
|-|-|
|COMMON ([[Rarity]])|+1|
|UNCOMMON ([[Rarity]])|+2|
|RARE ([[Rarity]])|+3|
|EPIC ([[Rarity]])|+4|
|LEGENDARY ([[Rarity]])|+5|
|MYTHIC ([[Rarity]])|+6|
[[Magic Find]] thresholds:
|Pet Score|[[Magic Find]]|
|-|-|
|10|+1 [[Magic Find]]|
|25|+2 [[Magic Find]]|
|50|+3 [[Magic Find]]|
|75|+4 [[Magic Find]]|
|100|+5 [[Magic Find]]|
|130|+6 [[Magic Find]]|
|175|+7 [[Magic Find]]|
|225|+8 [[Magic Find]]|
|275|+9 [[Magic Find]]|
|325|+10 [[Magic Find]]|
|375|+11 [[Magic Find]]|
|450|+12 [[Magic Find]]|
|500|+13 [[Magic Find]]|
## List of Pets
There are currently 87 pets available on the main server (86 on non-[[Bingo|Ⓑ Bingo]] profiles), each granting a variety of different benefits and advantages.
Some important notes:
- Important: Pet stats are increased every level, but the number displayed in the tooltip is always rounded down. This means that, if a pet gives +3.75 [[Crit Damage]], the number displayed in the tooltip will be 3. On the other hand, the [[Profile|SkyBlock Profile]] will display numbers rounded properly.
- Having a specific collection is required only to craft the pet. Players can use every pet without any restrictions.

## Pet Items
- [[Pet Items]] are items that can boost the benefits given by pets.
- Each pet can hold only one Pet Item at a time.
- Pet Items can be applied by right-clicking them onto an active, physically summoned pet (not from the Pets Menu).
- Pet Items are free to apply but cost [[Coins]] to be retrieved. This can be done:
  - Using a [[Super Scrubber]], without applying a new Pet Item.
  - Using any Pet Item to replace the currently equipped one.
- [[Zog]] sells various Pet Items in exchange for [[Coins]], most of which can be upgraded with a [[Zog Anvil]]. Other Pet Items can be obtained from the [[Mythological Ritual]], event rewards, or mob drops.
|Rarity|Cost|
|-|-|
|COMMON ([[Rarity]])|25,000 [[Coins]]|
|UNCOMMON ([[Rarity]])|50,000 [[Coins]]|
|RARE ([[Rarity]])|100,000 [[Coins]]|
|EPIC ([[Rarity]])|250,000 [[Coins]]|
|LEGENDARY ([[Rarity]])|500,000 [[Coins]]|
|MYTHIC ([[Rarity]])|1,000,000 [[Coins]]|
|DIVINE ([[Rarity]])|2,500,000 [[Coins]]|
## Pet Leveling
### Normal Pets
#### Skill Experience
- Summoned pets can be leveled up to [Lv100], with the exception of the [[Golden Dragon Pet]], [[Jade Dragon Pet]], and [[Rose Dragon Pet]], which can go to [Lv200].
- Pets gain experience from the players gaining Skill Experience with the pet equipped, with the following exceptions:
  - The [[Wisp Pet]], which is leveled up through feeding it [[Y]] [[Gabagool (disambiguation)|Gabagool]]
  - The [[Carpentry]], [[Taming]], [[Dungeoneering]], [[Runecrafting]] and [[Social]] skills do not give pet experience.
- Multiple factors affect the pet experience gained, they stack additively or multiplicatively. Additive factors are applied first.
  - Additively: [[Wisdom]] is a type of [[Stats|stat]] that increases the [[Skills|Skill]] XP the player gains for a specific [[Skills|Skill]].
  - Multiplicatively: these are compatible with [[Y]] [[Gabagool (disambiguation)|Gabagool]]. Pets can also gain experience with Exp Sharing.
Multiplicative factors:
|Pet XP multiplier|Type of source|Source|Condition|
|-|-|-|-|
|1.01× → 1.60×|Skill ([[Skills]])|[[Taming]] level (+1% Pet XP per level)||
|1.5×|Skill ([[Skills]])|Fishing XP ([[Fishing]])||
|1.001 → 1.050×|Accessory ([[Accessories]])|[[Beastmaster Crest]]||
|1.01 → 1.10×|Attribute ([[Attributes]])|Battle Experience ([[Attributes]]) ( [[Falcon Shard]])|Combat XP ([[Combat]])|
|1.1 → 1.5×|[[Pet Items]]|Some [[Pet Items]]|Most pet items only affect Pet XP gain from a specific Skill ([[Skills]]).|
|2×|Pet ([[Pets]]) perk|[[Reindeer Pet]]'s Winter Sprint perk||
|1.35×|Mayor ([[Mayor Election]]) perk|[[Diana]]'s Pet XP Buff perk||
|/3|Skill ([[Skills]])|Skill XP|Skill XP is different than the Pet Type Can compound with the below for a 1/12 multiplier.|
|/4|Skill ([[Skills]])|Alchemy XP ([[Alchemy]]) and Enchanting XP ([[Enchanting]])|Skill XP is different than the Pet Type. Can compound with the above for a 1/12 multiplier.|
#### Pet Food
Another way to level pets is by feeding them:
- [[Candy (disambiguation)|Carrot Candies]] exist in 4 variants, each giving different amounts of pet experience:
  - [[Simple Carrot Candy|Simple]], [[Great Carrot Candy|Great]] and [[Superb Carrot Candy|Superb]] [[Candy (disambiguation)|Carrot Candies]] are unlocked from [[Carrot]] collection at tiers 2, 6 and 8 respectively.
  - [[Ultimate Carrot Candy|Ultimate Carrot Candies]] are crafted from 8 [[Superb Carrot Candy|Superb Carrot Candies]] and 1 [[Ultimate Carrot Candy Upgrade]], the latter bought from [[Elizabeth]] for 8,000 [[Bits]] at the [[Community Center]].
  - Pets can consume a maximum of 10 [[Candy (disambiguation)|Carrot Candies]] during their lifetime. However, [[Ultimate Carrot Candy|Ultimate Carrot Candies]] fed via the [[Caducous Feeder]] do not count towards this limit.
- [[Chyme]] gives 90,000 Pet Experience and is obtained from [[Aranya]] on the [[Crimson Isle]].
- [[Pet Cake]] gives 10,000 Pet experience and is obtained from [[Oringo]] on [[The Garden]].
- [[Fish Food]] gives varying amounts of Pet experience and is obtained by losing a [[Loudmouth Bass]] duel.
|Tier|Pet XP gained|
|-|-|
|[[Simple Carrot Candy]]|20,000|
|[[Great Carrot Candy]]|200,000|
|[[Superb Carrot Candy]]|750,000|
|[[Ultimate Carrot Candy]]|1,500,000|
|[[Ultimate Carrot Candy]] (via [[Caducous Feeder]])|1,650,000|
|[[Chyme]]|90,000|
|[[Pet Cake]]|10,000|
|[[Fish Food]]|Varies|
#### Pet Training
Players can give their pets to [[Fann]] to gain Pet XP over time. This amount can be increased by paying more [[Coins]] and [[Bits]] (assuming the player has the required [[Taming]] level). It can also be increased by placing an [[Owl Pet]] along with the pet and syphoning [[Boreal Owl Shard|Boreal Owl Shards]].
Players can train multiple pets by feeding [[Fann|Hootie]] with 5 [[Great Carrot Candy]] and 1 [[Ultimate Carrot Candy]]. [[Enchanted Clock]] can be used to fast-forward the training by 1 hour. This effect has a 48 hour cooldown.
|Training Type|Cost/Day|Pet XP/Day|Pet XP/Day (+30% XP)|
|-|-|-|-|
|Free||20,000-50,000|26,000-65,000|
|Light|200,000 [[Coins]]|250,000|325,000|
|Moderate|1,000,000 [[Coins]]|500,000|650,000|
|Expert|2,000,000 [[Coins]]|750,000|975,000|
|Ultra|2,750,000 [[Coins]]|1,500,000|1,950,000|
|Turbo!|3,000,000 [[Coins]]; 500 [[Bits]]|2,500,000|3,250,000|

### Gabagool Pets
Gabagool Pets do not level up through Skill experience gain, and instead gain experience by being fed [[Gabagool (disambiguation)|Gabagool]]. This is still affected by [[Taming]], [[Beastmaster Crest]], [[Attributes|Battle Experience]], and [[All Skills Exp Boost]] or [[All Skills Exp Super-Boost]]. Currently the only Gabagool Pet is the [[Wisp Pet]].
|Tier|Pet Exp Gained|XP per Crude Gabagool|

|[[Crude Gabagool]]|100|100|
|[[Fuel Gabagool]]|3,200|133.34|
|[[Very Crude Gabagool]]|19,200|100|
|[[Heavy Gabagool]]|102,400|177.78|
|[[Hypergolic Gabagool]]|3,276,800|474.07|

### Upgrading Rarity
There are 3 ways to increase a Pet's rarity: [[Kat]], the [[Tier Boost]] Pet Item, and Pet-specific conditions.
- [[Kat]] resides at the front of the [[Pet Care]]. Given a Pet and varying amounts of [[Coins]] time and items, she will upgrade its rarity by 1 tier:
  - The [[Coins]] cost can be decreased: for each Pet Level, an additive discount of 0.3% is applied, up to 30% at [Lv100].
  - The time cost can be decreased by the following: (A) offering her [[Kat Flower|Kat Flowers]] (-1 day per) or [[Kat Bouquet|Kat Bouquets]] (-5 days per), both purchased from the [[Bits Shop]] for 500 [[Bits]] or 2,500 [[Bits]] respectively; (B) using an [[Enchanted Clock]] (-1 hour with a cooldown of 48 hours). (C) syphoning the [[Kiwi Shard]] ([[Attributes|Kat's Favorite]])
  - The upgraded pet will have its level adjusted based on its XP. For example, a [Lv86] [[Rarity|RARE]] pet with 4.6 million cumulative will be adjusted to [Lv81] when it is upgraded to [[Rarity|EPIC]].
  - Some Pets cannot be upgraded this way: [[Grandma Wolf Pet]], [[Montezuma Pet]], [[Rift Ferret Pet]], [[Kuudra Pet]], [[Wisp Pet]] and [[Bingo Pet]].
- Using a [[Tier Boost]] Pet Item will boost the rarity of a pet by 1 tier (similar to a [[Recombobulator 3000]]) until the [[Pet Items|Pet Item]] held by the Pet is changed.
- Some Pet-specific conditions exist:
    - The [[Grandma Wolf Pet]] is upgraded exclusively [Confirm (Confirmation needed; When this info is confirmed, remove this.)] through Bestiary Milestones
    - The [[Kuudra Pet]] is upgraded through [[Kuudra Chunk|Kuudra Chunks]]
    - The [[Bingo Pet]] is upgraded through [[Bingo|Ⓑ Bingo]] ranks
    - The [[Wisp Pet]] is upgraded exclusively through [[Wisp Upgrade Stone|Wisp Upgrade Stones]]
    - The [[Montezuma Pet]] is upgraded exclusively through finding [[Enigma Souls]].
### Pet Selling
Pets can be sold directly to [[George]], located at the [[Pet Care]], for [[Coins]] based on their type and [[Rarity]].
Selling each of the pets below to [[George]] for the first time at the listed rarity or higher increases the player's [[Taming]] level cap by 1, up to 60. The order does not matter.
- [[Rarity|EPIC]] [[Rift Ferret Pet]]
- [[Rarity|EPIC]] [[Slug Pet]]
- [[Rarity|EPIC]] [[Spirit Pet]]
- [[Rarity|EPIC]] [[Giraffe Pet]]
- [[Rarity|EPIC]] [[Jellyfish Pet]]
- [[Rarity|EPIC]] [[Bal Pet]]
- [[Rarity|EPIC]] [[Baby Yeti Pet]]
- [[Rarity|LEGENDARY]] [[Black Cat Pet]]
- [[Rarity|RARE]] [[Wisp Pet|Frost Wisp Pet]]
- [[Rarity|MYTHIC]] [[Enderman Pet]]
## Trivia
- If the player's inventory is full and a slain mob drops a pet, the pet will drop on the ground instead of going into the stash.
- Patch 0.7.8 was released on May 6, 2020, and contained multiple references and resemblances to Pokémon.
  - [[Pet Items]] resembled the ability to hold items in Pokémon. The [[Candy (disambiguation)|Carrot Candies]] resemble Rare Candies and simulate the same effect of giving XP to the Pokémon in the Pokémon games.
  - [[Kat]], one of the NPCs that got added in addition to the Pet update, has an appearance similar to that of Serena, the female protagonist from Pokémon X and Y.
- Four achievements can be earned related to pets.
  - Getting a pet to level 80 will award the "[[Achievements|Caretaker]]" achievement.
  - The "[[Achievements|Friend for Life]]" achievement is an extended version of the "Caretaker" achievement that is awarded upon taking a pet to level 100.
    - This can also be earned by purchasing a level 100 pet from an auction or obtaining a level 100 pet through a trade.
  - Having 20 unique pets awards "[[Achievements|The Real Zoo Shady]]" achievement.
  - Getting at least 100 Pet Score awards "[[Achievements|King Of The Pets]]" achievement.
- The highest amount of Pet EXP multiplier is 10.206x, which occurs with [[Taming|Taming LX (60)]], [[Diana]]'s Pet XP Buff, a maxed [[Beastmaster Crest]], an [[Rarity|EPIC]] [[Fishing Exp Boost]] and the player gets [[Fishing|Fishing XP]] while having a [[Reindeer Pet]] equipped.
  - For non-Reindeer Fishing pets and the [[Bingo Pet]], the number is 5.103x. For Mining pets, due to lack of [[Rarity|EPIC]] [[Mining Exp Boost]], it's 4.7628x. For Combat, Foraging or Farming pets the number is 3.402x. For Enchanting, Alchemy and Gabagool pets the number is 2.7216x. For Taming pets it's 0.9072x.
- There are several [[NPC|NPCs]] with their own Pets floating around them:
  - [[Bea]]: [[Bee Pet|LEGENDARY Lv90 Bee Pet]]
  - [[Lone Adventurer]]: [[Enderman Pet|LEGENDARY Lv46 Enderman Pet]], seen during his questline.
  - [[Lone Adventurer]]: [[Ender Dragon Pet|LEGENDARY Lv100 Ender Dragon Pet]], seen after his questline.
  - [[Spider Tamer]]: [[Tarantula Pet|Lv1 Tarantula Pet]], seen after obtaining his [[Abiphones|Abiphone]] Contact.
  - [[Vincent]]: [[Mole Pet|Lv53 Mole Pet]], seen after obtaining his [[Abiphones|Abiphone]] Contact.
  - [[Forager Rowan]]: [[Sloth Pet|EPIC Lv61 Sloth Pet]]

# Pity and RNG Meters
Aliases: Meter, Meters, Pities, Pity, RNG Meter, RNGesus Meter, Rng, Rngmeter
Categories: Pages with UIs, Outdated pages, More info needed
[Pities & RNG Meters]
Type: Mechanics
RNG (Random Number Generator) Meters and Pity are mechanics that prevent players from getting too unlucky, akin to a "Luck Insurance".
## Overview
||RNG Meters|Pities|
|-|-|-|
|Access from [[Commands]]|/rng, /rngmeter or /meter|/pity|
|Access from Widgets|✔|✔|
|Metric|Custom XP formulas|Number of repetitions of a task|
|Number of targets|1 per RNG Meter, selected by the player.|All active at once without need for selection.|
|Drop rate of target|1x→3x increasing linearly as it fills up. 100% once full.|Unchanged as it fills up. 100% once full.|
|Behavior upon obtaining a target|XP "price" for the target is subtracted. If there is not enough XP, it is set to 0.|Number of repetitions is set to 0 for that target.|
Some exceptions evade to these "rules" in both systems, as inconsistencies appeared throughout updates:
- For RNG Meters: in the case of Slayer Bosses, they do not affect the Drop Rates of items in the [[Slayer|Separate Loot Pools]] until the meter is full;
- For Pities: in the case of Glacite Mineshafts, they do affect their [[Glacite Mineshafts|appearance rate]].
## RNG Meter
The RNG Meter is a mechanic that translates the time and efforts spent in certain parts of SkyBlock into a currency named RNG Meter XP. That currency can be put to contribution towards a target of the player choosing, increasing its odds. Upon obtainment of the target, a "price" will be deducted from the stored RNG Meter XP.
### Types of RNG Meters
5 types of RNG Meters currently exist, each with their respective ways of contribution:
- 1 [[Experimentation Table]] RNG Meter, from 0.1% of [[Enchanting]] XP gained from [[Experiments|Superpairs]] experiments.
- 6 [[Slayer]] RNG Meters, from Slayer XP obtained from killing their respective Tier III or higher [[Slayer]] bosses.
- 14 [[Catacombs]] RNG Meters, from [[Dungeon Score]] of completed runs at 70% efficiency for S or at 100% efficiency for S+.
- 1 [[Crystal Nucleus]] RNG Meter, from Nucleus XP of completed [[Crystal Nucleus|Nuclei Runs]], each granting +1000.
- 1 [[Frozen Corpses]] RNG Meter, from Frozen Corpse XP of looted [[Frozen Corpses]], at +500 per Lapis corpse, +2,500 per Umber/Tungsten corpse, and +25,000 per Vanguard corpse.
### RNG Meter Boosts
RNG Meter XP can also be increased in the following ways:
- globally, by a flat amount depending on the chosen meter from consuming an [[Ananke Feather]] or an [[RNG Juice]]. The values are listed below.
- globally, by 1.01→1.10x from the [[Attributes|Pity]] attribute ( [[Daemon Shard]]). This does not affect [[Ananke Feather|Ananke Feathers]] nor [[RNG Juice]].
- on the [[Experimentation Table]], by a varying multiplier as [[Enchanting Wisdom]] and other [[Enchanting]] XP modifiers directly affect it.
|RNG Meter ([[Pity and RNG Meters]])|RNG Meter XP Granted [[Ananke Feather]]|RNG Meter XP Granted [[RNG Juice]]|

### Effect of RNG Meter progress on a selected drop
The formula of the drop rate of an item with the RNG Meter selected is:
  - New Drop Rate=Base Drop Rate×(1+min⁡(2×Stored RNG Meter XPRequired RNG Meter XP),2)
- If the Stored RNG Meter XP is above Required RNG Meter XP, then:
  - the drop is guaranteed;
  - the Required RNG Meter XP is subtracted from the Stored RNG Meter XP.
- In the event a player obtains the targeted drop in its RNG Meter:
  - If the Stored RNG Meter XP was below Required RNG Meter XP, then it is reset to 0.
  - If the Stored RNG Meter XP was above Required RNG Meter XP, then the Required RNG Meter XP is subtracted from it.
- In the event a player obtains a drop that was not targeted in its RNG Meter:
  - the RNG Meter progress is unaffected.
#### In the case of Slayer RNG Meters
- If a drop has its New Drop Rate<5%, then it is increased by [[Magic Find]].
- Note that every slayer drop other than guaranteed drops from a full RNG Meter will display the player's [[Magic Find]] in the chat message, even if the drop was not actually affected by it.
### Slayer RNG Meters

This article was last edited by Lunaynx (contribs|logs|block) on 3:06, October 26 2026 (22 hours ago)
[[Slayer|Slayers]] RNG Meters are filled with Slayer XP from defeating Tier III or higher [[Slayer]] bosses, with each slayer having its own independent Meter, for a total of 6.
- Items in the [[Slayer|Slayers]] RNG Meters have amounts of Slayer XP required to guarantee to roll, as per the formulas below. The Required Slayer XP for each item can be read in the further down.
  - Required Slayer XP=Slayer Multiplier×100Highest Base Percent Drop Rate
  - Slayer Multiplier={250for Vampire Slayer500for all other Slayer types
  - Highest Base Percent Drop Rate=max⁡(Percent Drop Rate for completed tier A,Percent Drop Rate for completed tier B,Percent Drop Rate for completed tier C,…); - For each tier of a [[Slayer]] boss defeated, the item's Percent Drop Rate is collected from its pool. Then, the highest of these is stored as the Highest Base Percent Drop Rate.
  - Percent Drop Rate for completed tier X={100if Token100×Item Weight∑𝐓𝐨𝐤𝐞𝐧Weight+∑𝐌𝐚𝐢𝐧Weightsif Main100×Item Weight∑𝐓𝐨𝐤𝐞𝐧Weights+∑𝐌𝐚𝐢𝐧Weights+∑𝐄𝐱𝐭𝐫𝐚Weightsif Extra; - Token drops (or Slayer Tokens) are not used in the RNG Meters as they cannot be selected as a target drop, despite being tied to a formula. Only drops that are unlocked are accounted for their weight, meaning the Percent Drop Rate of an item can be inflated from a low [[Slayer]] level.
1. The targeted item's Item Weight in that Loot Pool is then altered as per the following formula:
  - New Weight1=Item Weight×(1+min⁡(2×Stored Slayer XPRequired Slayer XP,2))
  For untargeted items, New Weight1=Item Weight.
1. If an item's New Weight1<5%, then it will be modified according to the following formula:
  - New Weight2=New Weight1×(1+Magic Find100)
  Otherwise, New Weight2=New Weight1.
- The menus can be accessed through [[Maddox the Slayer]] or the [[SkyBlock Menu]]. To view a specific [[Slayer]] RNG Meter, the player must have defeated its Tier III boss at least once.
  SkyBlock Menu ——> Your Skills ——> Combat ——> Slayer ——> Slayer Boss Type ——> RNG Meter
### Catacombs RNG Meters

[[Catacombs]] RNG Meters are filled with [[Dungeon Score]] of completed runs at 70% efficiency for S or at 100% efficiency for S+. Each floor has its own independent meter on REGULAR and [[Catacombs|MASTER]] modes except [[The Catacombs - Entrance|Entrance]], for a total of 14.
- Items in the Catacombs RNG Meters have amounts of Dungeon Score required to guarantee to roll, as per the formula below. The Required Dungeon Score for each item can be read in the list below.
  - Required Dungeon Score=300×Total WeightItem Weight
- The loot pool used is from the highest tier of [[Dungeon Reward Chest]] for that floor. The weight of the targeted item in that loot pool is then altered as per the following formula:
  - New Weight=Item Weight×(1+min⁡(2×Stored Dungeon ScoreRequired Dungeon Score,2))
- The menus can be accessed through the [[SkyBlock Menu]]. To view a specific floor RNG Meter, the player must have cleared it at least once.
  SkyBlock Menu ——> Your Skills ——> Dungeoneering ——> The Catacombs ——> Catacombs RNG Meters
### Crystal Nucleus RNG Meter
The Crystal Nucleus RNG Meter increases the drop rate of certain items from the list below.
- Placing all crystals at the Crystal Nucleus gains 1,000 Nucleus XP for the meter. Retroactive completions from before the release of the Crystal Nucleus RNG Meter grant 250 Nucleus XP rounded towards the nearest thousand.
- Items in the Crystal Nucleus RNG Meter have predefined amounts of XP required to guarantee to roll.
- The amount of XP is roughly based on drop chance. The XP values can be read in the list below.
- When a bundle is rolled, the selected item has an increased weight with the following formula. If enough XP is stored within the Crystal Nucleus RNG Meter, the selected item is guaranteed to drop.
  - New Weight=Item Weight×(1+min⁡(2×Stored Crystal Nucleus XPRequired Crystal Nucleus XP,2))
- The menu can be accessed through the [[SkyBlock Menu]]: [[SkyBlock Menu]] ——> [[Skills|Your Skills]] ——> [[Mining]] ——> [[Heart of the Mountain]] ——> [[Pity and RNG Meters|RNG Meter]]

### Frozen Corpses RNG Meter
The Frozen Corpses RNG Meter increases the drop rate of certain items from the list below.
- Looting a [[Frozen Corpses|Frozen Corpse]] grants varying amounts of Frozen Corpse XP depending on its type:
  - +500 per Lapis corpse;
  - +2,500 per Umber corpse;
  - +2,500 per Tungsten corpse;
  - +25,000 per Vanguard corpse.
- Items in the Frozen Corpse RNG Meter have predefined amounts of Frozen Corpse XP required to guarantee to roll. The amount of Frozen Corpse XP is roughly based on drop chance.
- When a bundle is rolled, the selected item has an increased weight with the following formula. If enough Frozen Corpse XP is stored within the Frozen Corpse RNG Meter, the selected item is guaranteed to drop.
  New Weight=Item Weight×(1+min⁡(2×Stored Frozen Corpse XPRequired Frozen Corpse XP,2))
- The menu can be accessed through [[Grandpa Wolf]] or the /corpsemilestones command: /corpsemilestones ——> [[Pity and RNG Meters|RNG Meter]]
