## Pity
Pity is a mechanic that guarantees an event or an item after a task has been repeated a certain number of times. Unlike RNG Meters and most of the time:
- there is no currency to be spent;
- there is no choice to be made as all targets are active and are covered by independent counters;
- there is no increase in the drop rate of targets.
- if the player hits the target from luck or Pity, its respective counter is reset to 0.
Again, these are not rules set in stone and exceptions occasionally evade them.
Several groups of Pity currently exist, each with their respective ways of contribution:
### Hoppity's Hunt
After opening x [[Chocolate Rabbits|Chocolate Eggs]], guarantees the next one to contain a new [[Chocolate Rabbits|Chocolate Rabbit]] of a given rarity, if available:
- [[Rarity|MYTHIC]]: 250 [[Chocolate Rabbits|Chocolate Eggs]]. Subtracts 250 from that counter.
- [[Rarity|DIVINE]]: 1,250 [[Chocolate Rabbits|Chocolate Eggs]]. Subtracts 1,250 from that counter. If the player has found all [[Rarity|DIVINE]] Rabbits, it guarantees a [[Chocolate Rabbits|Chocobit Rabbit]] instead.
### Year of the Pig
After completing x [[Shiny Pig|Shiny Pigs]], guarantees the next one to drop an item.
- [[Blood God Crest]]: 1,000 [[Shiny Pig|Shiny Pigs]]
- [[Potato Talisman]]: 2,000 [[Shiny Pig|Shiny Pigs]], both affected by the effect of the [[Pig Brain Mixin]].
### The End
After 400 combined [[Zealot|Zealots]] and [[Zealot Bruiser|Zealot Bruisers]] kills, guarantees the next mob kill to spawn a [[Special Zealot]].
### Crimson Isle
- [[Trophy Fish]]: After 100 or 600 catches of a specific [[Trophy Fish]], guarantees that the next catch will be GOLD or DIAMOND tier respectively.
- [[Vanquisher|Vanquishers]]: After 750 kills, guarantees the next one to spawn a [[Vanquisher]].
### Lotus Atoll
After 100 or 600 catches of a specific [[Trophy Frogs|Trophy Frog]], guarantees that the next catch will be of GOLD or DIAMOND tier respectively. These values are halved for [[Trophy Frogs|Exploding Frog]] and [[Puddle Jumper]].
### Garden
- [[Spaceman]]: After 3,000 visitors, guarantees that the next visitor will be the [[Spaceman]].
- [[Overclocker 3000]]: 5 activities increment their respective counter for an [[Overclocker 3000]] : [[Garden Visitors|Visitors]], [[Jacob's Farming Contest|Jacob's Contests]], [[Crops (disambiguation)|Crops]], [[Greenhouse]], [[Pests]]. The values are only displayed as percentages[More Info Needed] (More infomation needed; When this info is added, remove this.).
### Moonglade Marsh
- After 1,000 [[Tree Gifts]], 2,500 mob kills, or 2,500 [[Treasure|treasures]] respectively, guarantees the next action to give [[Tree the Fish]], [[Mob the Fish]], or [[Swamp the Fish]] respectively.
- Mobs killed in [[Torrhus Canyon]] also count toward [[Mob the Fish]] progress.
### Torrhus Canyon
After 2,500 [[Treasure|treasures]], guarantees the next action to give [[Fish the Fish]].
### Glacite Tunnels
[[Glacite Mineshafts]] are a special case as Pity works differently:
- The counter starts from 2,000, decreasing according to the Block Quality of the block mined. Once the counter reaches 0, the next block is guaranteed to spawn a [[Glacite Mineshafts|Glacite Mineshaft]] portal.
- If a block is mined from [[Mining Spread]] or [[Heart of the Mountain|Pickobolus]], its Block Quality is halved.
  - For example, if a player with 300 [[Mining Spread]] mines a [[Glacite]] block, 3 nearby ones will be indirectly mined. In total, the counter decreases by 10: once by 4 and thrice by 2.
- The counter determines the chance of a [[Glacite Mineshafts|Glacite Mineshaft]] portal spawning on the next block mined: it is the inverse of the counter.
- However, on the tab list and in the Pity menu, the counter appears as starting from 0, increasing to 2000 in contrary to the mechanics initially explained on its introduction
  - For example, if the in-game Pity counter appears at 859, the actual value is 2000-859=1141. Thus, the probability of spawning a [[Glacite Mineshafts|Glacite Mineshaft]] portal on the next block is 0,088% (1 in 1141 blocks).
  - For example, if the in-game Pity counter appears at 1995, the actual value is 2000-1995=5. Thus, the probability of spawning a [[Glacite Mineshafts|Glacite Mineshaft]] portal on the next block is 20% (1 in 5 blocks).

|Titanium Ores ([[Titanium]])|8|
|Umber Ores ([[Umber]])|4|
|Tungsten Ores ([[Tungsten]])|4|
|Glacite Ores ([[Glacite]])|4|
|Ruby Gemstones ([[Gemstone]]); Sapphire ([[Gemstone]]), Jade ([[Gemstone]]), Amber ([[Gemstone]]), Amethyst Gemstones ([[Gemstone]]); Topaz Gemstones ([[Gemstone]])|8|
|Aquamarine ([[Gemstone]]), Peridot ([[Gemstone]]), Citrine ([[Gemstone]]), Onyx Gemstones ([[Onyx (disambiguation)]])|10|
|Other (such as [[Hard Stone]])|0|
## Trivia
- Contrary to popular belief, the [[Pity and RNG Meters|RNG Meter]]'s formula is indeed applied to all rolls of a [[Crystal Nucleus|Crystal Nucleus Bundle]].

# Potion Bag
Aliases: Giant Potion Bag, Large Potion Bag, Massive Potion Bag, Medium Potion Bag, Small Potion Bag
Categories: Pages with UIs, Upgrades, Nether Wart Collection, SkyBlock Menu
[Potion Bag]
Type: Storage upgrade
Appearance: Vial with a rainbow liquid inside
Collection: Nether Wart II (2) ([[Nether Wart]])
Usage: Store Potions and Mixins
[Capacity]
Small: 9 slots
Medium: 18 slots
Large: 27 slots
Giant: 36 slots
Massive: 45 slots
The Potion Bag allows players to store [[Potions]] and [[Mixins]]. It could be accessed from the [[SkyBlock Menu]] and is unlocked at [[Nether Wart|Nether Wart II (2)]].
## Obtaining
The Potion Bag is unlocked at [[Nether Wart|Nether Wart II (2)]] and is upgraded throughout the Collection.
|Collection|Size|
|-|-|
|Nether Wart II (2) ([[Nether Wart]])|9 slots|
|Nether Wart V (5) ([[Nether Wart]])|18 slots|
|Nether Wart VIII (8) ([[Nether Wart]])|27 slots|
|Nether Wart X (10) ([[Nether Wart]])|36 slots|
|Nether Wart XII (12) ([[Nether Wart]])|45 slots|
## Tips
- [[Potions|Dungeon Potions]] bought from [[Ophelia]] or dropped from [[The Watcher|Speedy Watcher summons]] can be stored in the Potion Bag. This is useful for players who use Dungeon Potions or have no Inventory space to store the potions.
## History
[[SkyBlock Prototype]]:
|Jun 11, 2019|0.1|Added Potion Bag.|
|Jun 2, 2020|0.7.9|Added Giant and Massive Potion Bag upgrades.|
|Mar 9, 2021|0.11.2|Potion Bag can now store God Potions ([[God Potion]]).|
|Jul 22, 2026|0.26.1|Potion Bag no longer has Small, Medium, Large, Giant, Massive Prefixes.|
|Oct 6, 2026|0.27.2|Potion Bag can now store [[Mixins]].|

# Potions
Aliases: Alchemy XP Boost Potion, Brewing, Combat XP Boost Potion, Combat Xp Boost, Combat xp boost, Effects, Enchanting XP Boost Potion, Farming XP Boost Potion, Fishing XP Boost Potion, Foraging XP Boost Potion, Instant Health Potion, King's Scent Potion, Mining XP Boost Potion, Potion, Potion Effect, Potion Effects, Potion effect, Potion effects, Regeneration, Skill XP Boost Potion, Splash, Splash (disambiguation), Splashes, Wisp's Ice-Flavored Water I Splash Potion
Categories: Pages with UIs, More info needed, Article stubs, Templates with deprecated argument names, Potions, Confirmations needed, Brewing, Alchemy
Potions are consumable liquids which are brewed in [[Brewing Stand|Brewing Stands]], granting Potion Effects when consumed. Potion Effects are effects that grants the player positive effects and stat boosts (known as Buffs) or negative effects and stats (known as Debuffs).

## Brewing
A [[Brewing Stand]] is required to make potions, and it can be bought from the [[Alchemist]] for 30 [[Coins]] each, or crafted with 3 [[Cobblestone]] and 1 [[Blaze Rod]].
Potion brewing is similar to vanilla brewing in the following ways:
- [[Nether Wart]] turns [[Water Bottle|Water Bottles]] into [[Awkward Potion|Awkward Potions]]
- [[Glowstone Dust]] increases its potency
- [[Redstone Dust]] increases its duration
- [[Gunpowder]] turns Drunkable Potions in their Splash variant.
There are some notable differences to how brewing works in [[SkyBlock]], such as:
- Brewing does not require [[Blaze Powder]]
- [[Glowstone Dust|Glowstone upgrades]] and [[Redstone Dust|Redstone upgrades]] can be stacked on each other, but only one of each kind
- Enchanted ingredients will result in stronger and/or longer potions.
- Potion duration is affected by the brewer's [[Alchemy]] Level
- The brewing stand uses a custom UI:
  - Potions are brewed by placing Bottles or [[Brews]] in the lower slots;
  - Ingredients are placed in the upper slot.
  - When a potion is brewing, the glass panes in the middle will flash orange and yellow. After 20 seconds, the potion will finish brewing ;
Most potions last 3 minutes by default, which is increased by [[Alchemy]] Level or Redstone ingredients, including the [[Enchanted Redstone Lamp]]. The default level of the potions depends on the base ingredient; enchanted forms give a higher base level, including the [[Enchanted Redstone Lamp]].
### Base Potions
|Icon|Potion|Obtained through|Usage|
|-|-|-|-|
|[[Water Bottle]]|[[Water Bottle]]|Water ([[Vanilla Objects/Blocks]]) or from [[Alchemist]]|No effect when drunk, used to make awkward potions and weakness potions|
|[[Awkward Potion]]|[[Awkward Potion]]|[[Nether Wart]] applied on a [[Water Bottle]]|No effect when drunk, used for other potions|
|[[Cheap Coffee]]/[[Tepid Green Tea]]/[[Pulpous Orange Juice]]/[[Bitter Iced Tea]]/[[KnockOff™ Cola]]/[[Decent Coffee]]/[[Viking's Tear]]|[[Brews]]|Purchased from the [[Bartender]], [[Shifty]], [[Grog]], [[Ezekiel]] and the [[Melancholic Viking]]|[[Brews]] can replace the [[Awkward Potion]] as a base in some potions to boost a specific stat of the potion. These boosts work only on potions that already have the stat or are specified for use, and any percentage boost is to the potion's stats, not the player's.|
### Modifiers
#### Ingredients
After brewing potions, players can enhance them by combining them with certain ingredients. All modifier types can be used together, but only one of each type will take effect.
|Ingredient|Duration|Level|Splash|
|-|-|-|-|
|[[Redstone Dust]]|8 minutes||✕|
|[[Enchanted Redstone Dust]]|16 minutes||✕|
|[[Enchanted Redstone Block]]|40 minutes||✕|
|[[Enchanted Redstone Lamp]]|16 minutes|+3|✕|
|[[Glowstone Dust]]||+1|✕|
|[[Enchanted Glowstone Dust]]||+2|✕|
|[[Enchanted Glowstone]]||+3|✕|
|[[Gunpowder]]|0.5×||✔|
|[[Enchanted Gunpowder]]|||✔|
- Duration Modifiers are applied before [[Alchemy]] Level, the [[Potion Affinity Talisman]] or its upgrades, and the [[Parrot Pet]]'s Repeat perk.
- Potions Levels are capped by their maximum level. Not all potions have the same maximum level.
#### Other Modifiers

## Potions and Effects
- Potions give time-limited Buffs (positive effects and stat boosts) or Debuffs (negative effects and stats) when used.
- Potions do not stack.
- Using a potion will set both the timer and level of that effect, to match that potion's ones, with 2 exceptions:
  - The drunk Potion grants a Potion Effect of a lower level than the active one;
  - The drunk Potion is a [[God Potion]], which takes precedence over all other Buff effects, and cannot be altered by taking a different duration nor level Buff potion.
- Entering [[Catacombs|The Catacombs]] pauses and stores all existing potion effects the player has. The player can then receive potion buffs separately within, but only 1 Potion may be consumed or splashed per instance.
- The [[God Potion]] grants most Buffs at their maximum level to players for 12 → 24 hours depending on their [[Alchemy]] level, and up to ≈29 hours with a [[Rarity|LEGENDARY]] [[Parrot Pet]].
### Potion Rarity
A potion's rarity is determined by its potion level.
|Level|I|II|III|IV|V|VI|VII|VIII|
|-|-|-|-|-|-|-|-|-|
|Rarity|COMMON ([[Rarity]])|COMMON ([[Rarity]])|UNCOMMON ([[Rarity]])|UNCOMMON ([[Rarity]])|RARE ([[Rarity]])|RARE ([[Rarity]])|EPIC ([[Rarity]])|EPIC ([[Rarity]])|
The only [[Rarity|SPECIAL]] potion is the [[God Potion]].
## List of Potion Effects
### Brewable Potions
Brewable potions can be created by the character on a Brewing Stand, although most non-vanilla potions have an unlock requirement to be allowed to brew them. The recipes for these can be found in-game in the [[Recipe Book]].

### Skill XP Boost Potions
This section needs more information. Please help expand it. Note: This is barebone
See more stubs.
These Potions can be obtained from [[Gifts]]. Each of these Potions' base attributes give 5 [[Wisdom]] for 12 minutes at Level Ⅰ, 10 [[Wisdom]] for 24 minutes at Level Ⅱ, and 20 [[Wisdom]] for 36 minutes at Level Ⅲ.
- Attempting to modify these on their own will result in the [[Brewing Stand]] not activating.
- At least one unmodified of these must also be on the [[Brewing Stand]] for modifiers to apply.
Redstone and Enchanted Redstone Dust cannot affect Skill XP Boost Potions.
Glowstone will increment an unmodified Skill XP Boost Potion's level by 1 (up to a maximum of level III), but will not affect the Potion's duration. Enchanted Glowstone will increment an unmodified Skill XP Boost Potion's level by 2 (up to a maximum of Level III), but will not affect the Potion's duration.
An Enchanted Redstone Lamp will change an unmodified Skill XP Boost Potion in the following ways: The Potion's level will be increased to/kept at III with a 20 [[Wisdom]] boost effect. The Potion's duration will be set to 36 minutes plus the brewer's Alchemy level bonus.

## Alchemy Experience
Brewing potions grants [[Alchemy|Alchemy XP]]. The base item used to brew a potion determines the amount of [[Alchemy|Alchemy XP]] which will be earned once the potion is removed from its [[Brewing Stand]].
Only the first ingredient after the [[Awkward Potion]] affects brewing leveling XP. In some cases, players can get a tier V potion that is not in the recipe book by adding [[Enchanted Glowstone]] to a Tier III potion, provided that the Tier V version can be made without modifiers. Although the result will be a Tier V potion, only the Tier III XP reward will be awarded.
The Cost-Effectiveness of a potion is calculated using the ingredient's purchasing price. If there is no purchasing price available, the material price will be used instead. If there is neither, then the theoretical purchasing price used for calculation is 2.5x the selling price.

## Splashes
Splashes were player-hosted events where someone used splash potions to give multiple people effects at once at a fraction of the cost-per-person.
Typically, a splasher would gather between 20 and 30 people in a party, and charge each person according to the types of potions; a full suite of positive effects would cost 90k [[Coins]], while more specific effects may cost less. After the party reached capacity, the splasher would warp everyone to an empty lobby and splash all the potions on them. This allowed the splasher to take a profit from brewing potions, while providing positive effects to individuals at a fraction of the cost.
With the 0.9 [[Community Center]] Update, these have decreased in popularity heavily due to the addition of the [[God Potion]] which can provide all these potion effects at a more efficient cost. However, it is still common for players to splash themselves and their teammates with a potion at the beginning of a [[Dungeons|Dungeon]], since their [[God Potion]] would be paused and inactive.

## Bingo
With the advent of the first [[Bingo]] being added December 1st, 2021, Community splashes were organized in an effort to make the week-long event easier for players. Since Elizabeth's Bits shop cannot be used on a Bingo profile, players had no access to the God Potion on a Bingo profile.
Although the addition of [[Alixer]] has provided all players with potion effects on Bingo, Community Splashes still remain. Community Splashes provide many potion effects that Alixer does not apply at any Bingo rank, as well as having a stronger effect.

## Tips
- The [[Potion Bag]] can be used to store potions and [[Mixins]], up to 45 at a time at the Massive tier.
- A [[Witch Pet]] can reduce the time of brewing Potions, down to 10 seconds.
- [[Haste Potion|Haste Potions]] function differently on the [[Private Island]], granting the vanilla [[Haste Potion|Haste]] effect.
- Some modifiers require a specific order to avoid issues:
  - [[Enchanted Gunpowder]] must be added after a duration modifier - applying it before will not activate its special effect, thus removing any benefit from using the Enchanted Gunpowder instead of normal Gunpowder.
  - If using both the [[Enchanted Redstone Block]] and [[Enchanted Redstone Lamp]] (but only for level increase, due to it's cheaper price), the Enchanted Redstone Block must be added first; doing it in the reverse will cause no extra effect. This is because the Lamp will fill both the duration and modifier slots once use, preventing a second modifier to be applied - but applying the duration modifier first allows the Lamp to only fill up the available level modifier.
  - The current value for [[Enchanted Redstone Lamp]] is and [[Enchanted Glowstone]] is . (Be sure to double-check the market price before buying).
- Currently, the best method of gaining [[Alchemy]] XP is brewing [[Speed Potion|Speed Potions]] with [[Enchanted Sugar Cane]] or brewing [[Weakness Potion|Weakness Potions]] with [[Enchanted Fermented Spider Eye]]. One [[Enchanted Sugar Cane]]/[[Enchanted Fermented Spider Eye]] yields 45,000 Alchemy Experience (15,000 per potion) and while [[Speed Potion]]s with normal [[Glowstone Dust|Glowstone]] sell for 13k total, [[Weakness Potion|Weakness Potions]] with [[Glowstone Dust|Glowstone]] sell for 39k total.

- The [[Secret I Potion|Secret Potion]] cannot be drank, and therefore provides no effects.

# Quests & Chapters
Categories: Pages with UIs, SkyBlock Menu
- This article is a work in progress.
- This article is incomplete and will be expanded on over the course of the article's creation.
- If you see missing, incorrect or out of date information, you are welcomed to edit this page.
This article was last edited by AAAHHHHAHHAAHAAH (contribs|logs|block) on 3:11, September 11 2026 (45 days ago)
Quests & Chapters is an option in the [[SkyBlock Menu]] represented by a [[Glitched Items|Book and Quill]] that allows for access to [[Chapters]] and [[Quests]].
## Usage
The Quests & Chapters menu has two main interactive buttons, one for accessing the [[Chapters]] menu and one for accessing the [[Quests]] menu.
## Interface
Tip: click on certain slots to change the UI or go to another article!
Linked from: [[Quests]], [[SkyBlock Menu]]

# Quiver
Aliases: Giant Quiver, Large Quiver
Categories: Pages with UIs, Upgrades, String Collection, Mechanics, SkyBlock Menu, Bows
[Quiver]
Type: Upgrade
Appearance: Pouch
Collection: String III (3) ([[String]])
Usage: Store Arrows
[Capacity]
Default: 27 slots
Large: 36 slots
Giant: 45 slots
The Quiver is a [[Bags|Bag]] in the [[SkyBlock Menu]] that can store different types of [[Arrows]], alongside [[Arrow Poison|Arrow Poisons]] and miscellaneous items (e.g. [[Slimeball]], [[Sulphur]]) that correspond to their specific bows. It is unlocked at [[String|String III (3)]].
## Obtaining
The Quiver can be upgraded through the [[String]] collection.
|Collection|Total Slots|
|-|-|
|String III (3) ([[String]])|27|
|String VI (6) ([[String]])|36|
|String IX (9) ([[String]])|45|
## Usage
The Quiver can hold up to 45 stacks of [[Arrows]], [[Toxic Arrow Poison]], [[Twilight Arrow Poison]], [[Prismarine Shard|Prismarine Shards]], [[Slimeball|Slimeballs]], [[Magma Cream]], and/or [[Sulphur]], which will be automatically used when firing a [[Bows|Bow]]. A stack of enchanted arrows will be shown instead of the [[SkyBlock Menu]] when a bow is held once this upgrade has been unlocked. If holding a [[Shortbows|shortbow]], a stack of feathers will be displayed instead.
Similarly to [[Sacks]], projectiles picked up will go into the quiver. [[Arrow Poison|Arrow Poisons]] do not go into the quiver automatically.
At shops which sell [[Flint Arrow|Flint Arrows]], a button will appear on the bottom right corner to fill the quiver with arrows. This fills all empty slots of the quiver with [[Flint Arrow|Flint Arrows]] for the listed cost.
## Trivia
- The Quiver cannot store [[Ender Pearl|Ender Pearls]] for the [[Ender Bow]].
## History
[[Alpha Hypixel Network]]:
|Jul 13, 2021|Added Clear Quiver button.|
[SkyBlock Prototype]
|Jun 11, 2019|0.1|Added Quiver.|
|Sep 11, 2019|0.7.1|Quiver arrow now replaces the [[SkyBlock Menu]] while holding a Bow ([[Bows]]).|
|Unknown Date|Quiver Arrows now appear as a stack of 64 rather than a stack of 1.|
|Jun 2, 2020|0.7.9|Added Large and Giant Quiver upgrades.|
|Sep 7, 2021|Added Clear Quiver button.|

# Resistance General
Aliases: 2nfg, 56ms, FurryEBoy, Refraction, ThirtyVirus, Thirtyvirus, TimeDeo
Categories: Pages with UIs, Non-player characters, The Uprising
[Resistance General]
FurryEBoy.png Better Together - If you are on a public island with a party member, gain +25% skill experience.
Type: [[NPC]]
56ms.png Infestation - Will introduce rats.
Type: [[NPC]]
TimeDeo.png Blessing of the Fairies - Stats gained from Fairy Souls are doubled!
Type: [[NPC]]
2nfg.png Why am I here - Lose 1 [[Intelligence]]
Type: [[NPC]]
thirtyvirus.png What did it cost - Adds the recipe for the Spiked Atrocity
Type: [[NPC]]
Refraction.png Incredible Luck - Grants everyone +20 [[Magic Find]]! Also grants +5% extra loot to end of dungeon chests.
Type: [[NPC]]
The resistance: Technoblade, his generals & Seraphine
The Resistance Generals were the six runners-up in the [[Resistance Movement]] leader election after the winner, 8Technoblade, who became the [[Resistance Leader]]. While the Leader received a full set of perks, the six Generals each received one. The Generals were 7FurryEBoy (now known as 7tylerwith4rs), 756ms, 12TimeDeo, 62nfg (who was banned), 7thirtyvirus, and 7Refraction.
After their victory over [[Dante]], the Generals (excluding 62nfg) became 8Technoblade's Ministers when he became [[Mayor Election|Mayor]].
## Location
Before the Uprising, the Generals could be found behind [[Clerk Seraphine|Seraphine]] in the [[Dark Auction]] on the basement stage. The Dark Auction house is located at [[Coordinates|91, 75, 176]] in the [[Wilderness]], near the [[Wilderness|Fairy Pond]]. After the Uprising, they moved to the [[Community Center]] and became Ministers, save for 62nfg.
## Interface
Tip: click on certain slots to change the UI or go to another article!
2nfg did not make it to Resistance General.
## Trivia
- As a General, 62nfg had iron bars surrounding him, referencing that he was banned when the Resistance Leader election ended.
  - This is likely why his perk is the only one with a negative effect: reducing all players' [[Intelligence]] by 1.
- The Generals were the first people to become Ministers, and the only ones to have the position before it was fully introduced in SkyBlock version 0.20.3.
  - This was also the first time that a Mayor had more than one Minister, and the only time until the [[Minister Election]] was added.
- Each General's perk is a callback about them:
  - Better Together - 7FurryEBoy's perk only affecting players on the same public island as a party member links to his YouTube channel being co-owned with Hellcastle and them playing as a duo.
  - Infestation - 756ms' perk is a reference to an ongoing joke about him being a rat. This is due to him playing a game called Escape from Tarkov in which he’d title streams "Rat Hunting", leading fans to start calling him a rat. His perk introduces [[Rat|Rats]] and the [[Rat Pet]].
  - Blessing of the Fairies - 12TimeDeo's perk boosting [[Fairy Souls|Fairy Soul]] stats is a reference to his popular guides on finding them.
  - Why am I here - 62nfg's perk name is a joke about him being elected for apparently no reason, considering he was banned at the time. He also never became a Minister.
  - What did it cost - 7thirtyvirus's perk name is a direct callback to the movie Avengers: Infinity War. The [[Spiked Atrocity]] originates from him calling the [[Spiked Bait]] "ThanoFish" in reference to the appearance of the film's antagonist, Thanos.
  - Incredible Luck - 7Refraction's perk is a joke on his unluckiness even with extremely high [[Magic Find]], resulting in him calling [[Magic Find]] useless multiple times. The extra loot in [[Dungeon Reward Chest|Dungeon Reward Chests]] is another joke on his unluckiness, as he very rarely drops good items in [[Dungeons]] on streams or in videos.
- Seraphine was a runner-up in the election, though it appears that this was a player, not the actual NPC.
## History
[[SkyBlock Prototype]]:
|Apr 12, 2021|Announced the voting results and added Resistance Generals.|
|Apr 16, 2021|Resistance Generals became Ministers after the Uprising.|

# Resistance Leader
Aliases: Mayor Technoblade, Technoblade
Categories: Pages with UIs, Shops, Non-player characters, The Uprising
[Technoblade]
Technoblade.png A picture of Technoblade, the one chosen by the Resistance to become the leader.
Also known as: Mayor Technoblade, Resistance Leader
Type: Mayor ([[Mayor Election]])
Shop: ✔ Yes
The Resistance Leader was the player that was elected to lead the [[Resistance Movement|Resistance]] against [[Dante]]. After [[Dante|The Uprising]], the Resistance Leader became the [[Mayor Election|mayor]].
## Information
8Technoblade was elected leader of the Resistance, alongside 6 [[Resistance General|generals]]: 756ms, 12TimeDeo, 7thirtyvirus, 62nfg, 7FurryEBoy, and 7Refraction. When the [[Resistance Movement|Resistance]] won, each of these provided a minor buff or new feature, with 8Technoblade's buffs being equivalent to that of a mayor's buffs.
After 8Technoblade became the mayor, the [[Piggy|Technoshop]] and [[Shiny Pig|Shiny Pigs]] were added to the game alongside his buffs.
## Dialogue
|Condition|Dialogue|
|-|-|
|In [[Dark Auction]] basement|[NPC] Resistance Leader: The sun will rise again!|
## Perks
Candidate Technoblade may run for election with up to 5 possible perks.
  [[Resistance Leader|Technoblade]]
1. Blood God's Blessing: +50% Skill XP
2. Shrine Of The Blood God: Summons the Blood God Shrine and its [[Shiny Pig|Shiny Pigs]] (This perk continued after Technoblade's term as the shiny relics were not all found)
3. Unlimited Speed: Gives +50 [[Speed]] and removes the speed cap (This perk was bugged and did not seem to work, thus being removed halfway through Technoblade’s mayor term)
4. Potato Crown's Radiance: Farming Minions have double output (This perk was bugged and seemed to work on all minions, rather than only farming minions)
5. Anarchy: There are no taxes in the [[Auction House]] and [[Bazaar]] (This perk was shortly removed after Technoblade was elected, likely that the admins thought no taxes would cause excessive inflation)
## Interface
Tip: click on certain slots to change the UI or go to another article!
## History
[[SkyBlock Prototype]]:
|Apr 12, 2021|Added Resistance Leader.|
|Apr 16, 2021|The Resistance Leader becomes Mayor ([[Mayor Election]]).|
|Apr 18, 2021|Removed 'Anarchy' perk.|
|Unknown Date|The Potato Crown's Radiance perk was changed from "Farming minions work 100% faster." to "Farming minions have double the output".|

# Reward Bundles
Aliases: 2019 Halloween Rewards Bundle, 2019 Summer Rewards Bundle, Reward Bundle, Rewards Bundle, Rewards Bundles

The Salesman
Reward Bundles are obtained by talking to an NPC near the [[Auction House]] while an event is active; if no event is active, the player will only see the [[Salesman]].
Reward bundles require a [[Ranks|Hypixel Rank]] to claim, with higher ranks unlocking more rewards. Rewards come in 3 types: [[Sprays]], [[Minion Skins]], and [[Furniture]].

# Rod Parts
Aliases: Fishing Hook, Fishing Rod Parts, Fishing hook, Fishing rod parts, Hook, Hooks, Line, Rod Part, Sinker
Categories: Pages with UIs, Fishing, Rod Parts
[Rod Parts]
Rod Parts are a core part of [[Fishing]]. They can be applied to [[Fishing Rods]] to grant various bonuses.
## Overview
[[Fishing Rods]] can have 3 Rod Parts applied at once. They can be applied at [[Roddy]] in the [[Backwater Bayou]] for various perks. There are 3 types of Rod Parts:
- Hooks increases the chance to catch certain items or [[Sea Creatures]].
- Lines grant stat bonuses.
- Sinkers grant [[Fishing Rods]] miscellaneous special abilities.
Only one type of Rod Part may be applied to a [[Fishing Rods|Fishing Rod]] at once. If the player wants to apply a different Rod Part, they need to remove the previous one first.
## List of Rod Parts

|-|-|-|-|-|-|
|[[Common Hook]]|[[Common Hook]]|COMMON ([[Rarity]])|Increases the chance to catch COMMON ([[Rarity]]) [[Sea Creatures]] by 25%.|Fishing X (10) ([[Fishing]])|Recipe: [[Enchanted Lily Pad]] ×2 + [[Enchanted Raw Cod]] → [[Common Hook]]|
|[[Puddle Jumper Hook]]|[[Puddle Jumper Hook]]|RARE ([[Rarity]])|Increases the chance to catch a [[Puddle Jumper]] by 400%.|Fishing XXV (25) ([[Fishing]])|Lotus VIII (8) ([[Lotus]]) Recipe: [[Gold Lotus]] ×2 + [[Enchanted Raw Cod]] x32 → [[Puddle Jumper Hook]]|
|[[Spooky Hook]]|[[Spooky Hook]]|EPIC ([[Rarity]])|Grants a +20% chance to catch Spooky Sea Creatures ([[Sea Creatures]]) during the [[Spooky Festival]].|Fishing XXI (21) ([[Fishing]])|1% chance to drop from [[Phantom Fisher]]|
|[[Hotspot Hook]]|[[Hotspot Hook]]|EPIC ([[Rarity]])|Grants a +20% chance to catch Hotspot Sea Creatures ([[Sea Creatures]]) while fishing in a Hotspot ([[Fishing Hotspots]]).|Fishing XX (20) ([[Fishing]])|Recipe: [[Condensed Lily Pad]] + [[Broken Radar]] x8 + [[Ink Splat]] x16 → [[Hotspot Hook]]|

|[[Trophy Line]]|[[Trophy Line]]|RARE ([[Rarity]])|Grants +5 [[Trophy Chance]] to [[Fishing Rods]].|Fishing XX (20) ([[Fishing]])|Lotus IX (9) ([[Lotus]]) Recipe: [[Gold Lotus]] x2 ×3 → [[Trophy Line]]|

## Trivia
- When the [[Phantom Rod]] was being removed in the version 0.22 Alpha, the Hypixel development team stated under the second day's changes that there are plans to add a new Sinker which applies the Phantom Rod's ability to any fishing rod. The Sinker with Phantom Rod's abilities has not currently released yet, as the team said it might take a while as it might necessitate nerfs to either the Phantom Impel ability or the [[Shredded Line]] in order to make them both compatible and balanced.

# Runecrafting
Aliases: Runic, Runic Mob
Categories: Pages with UIs, Potions, Skills, Runecrafting, Cosmetics
Runecrafting is one of the [[Skills]] available for players to level up by fusing runes, killing Runic Mobs, or by killing [[Bosses]]. It allows the player to fuse [[Runes]] onto their weapons and armor. Runecrafting is a cosmetic-only mechanic, and can only be leveled up to level 3 with the 1[[Ranks|Default]] rank.
[[Roman Numerals]] are used to express its levels in-game.
## Mechanics
Runecrafting is a cosmetic skill that has content locked to players without a [[Ranks|Rank]]. Players with no ranks cannot increase past level 3 in the skill, and higher ranks apply a higher exp multiplier. As a "cosmetic" skill no important content or any stats require it to be leveled, and other than some small benefits it may provide (such as tabbed chests) it's purely decoration. The other cosmetic skill is [[Social]].
Each rune has an associated level. The runes can be fused- however, they cannot be activated until the player has their Runecrafting level equal to or greater than the level of the rune.
### Runic Mobs
Runes are obtained from Runic Mobs. Mobs have a 0.5% (1 in 200) to be Runic. Runic Mobs have a purple name tag, 4x [[Health]], and deal 2x [[Damage]]. When killed, they are guaranteed to drop a Rune.
All mobs can be Runic except [[Ender Dragon]], [[Magma Cube Boss]], [[Slayer]] Boss/Miniboss, [[Catacombs Mobs|Dungeon Boss]], [[Catacombs Mobs|Dungeon Boss Minion]], [[Rift Dimension|Rift]] Mobs.
Certain runes are also obtained from [[Slayer|Slayers]] or as special rewards.
### Fusing Runes
Runes can be fused on the [[Runic Pedestal]], found behind the [[Blacksmith]] and owned by the NPC [[Dusk]].
Fusing two runes together will have a chance of upgrading the rune to the next level, up to the maximum level of 3. Upon success or failure, the second rune is consumed. If this process does not succeed, the remaining rune will not gain the level. The chance of success can be seen in the Runic Pedestal while hovering over the apply trigger. Regardless of the outcome, this will also grant Runecrafting experience. More experience is awarded upon success.
Runes can be fused onto Swords, [[Bows]], [[Chestplates]], and [[Boots]], depending on the type of rune. The player must have their Runecrafting level either equal to or greater than the level of the rune, or they won't be able to fuse it. This only applies to fuse runes to items; runes above the player's Runecrafting level can still be combined together. Fusing runes onto items will also grant Runecrafting experience.
## Experience Table
|Level|XP Required to Reach|Cumulative XP|
|-|-|-|
[0]
|1|50|50|
|2|100|150|
|3|125|275|
|4|160|435|
|5|200|635|

### Runecrafting EXP Multipliers
If you have different ranks on Hypixel, you will get more XP.
- Normal: x1.0, Max level 3
- 2[[Ranks|[VIP]]]: x1.1
- 3[[Ranks|[VIP+]]]: x1.25
- 4[[Ranks|[MVP]]]: x1.5
- 5[[Ranks|[MVP+]]]: x2
- 6[[Ranks|[MVP++]]]: x3
## Leveling Rewards
The cumulative rewards at Runecrafting Level XXV (25) are:
- Access to level 1-25 [[Runes|Runes.]]
## Bugs
- Sometimes, [[Depth Strider]] stops working on boots with a rune. The only workaround is to throw the boots onto the ground and pick it up.
- Players with the 1[[Ranks|Default]] rank are still able to gain Runecrafting XP beyond level 3 from killing runic mobs.
## Trivia
- This skill was supposed to be released July 16, 2019, but was delayed to August 2, 2019, due to the need for more testing.
- If a player with the 1[[Ranks|Default]] rank manages to reach Runecrafting IV (4) or above (e.g. from the above mentioned bug, or having their rank removed by Hypixel Support), they are able to use perks of the higher levels.
  - However, any player with the 1[[Ranks|Default]] rank who has 435 or more Runecrafting XP is generally automatically reset to 275 XP within an hour.
- The Tidal Rune is the only Rune that is in bold font.
- The Jerry Rune and the Grand Searing Rune are the only Runes that are only available in the 3rd tier.
- The Fiery Burst Rune is currently the only Rune that can be applied to Hoes.
## History
[[SkyBlock Prototype]]:
|Aug 2, 2019|0.7|Added Runecrafting.|
|Aug 27, 2022|Added [[Runecrafting Wisdom]].|

# Runic Pedestal
Aliases: Rune Pedestal
Categories: Pages with UIs, Runecrafting, Mechanics, Village
[Runic Pedestal]
The Runic Pedestal allows the [[Player]] to remove, apply, or upgrade [[Runes]]. It can be found inside the [[Blacksmith (Zone)|Blacksmith]] at [[Coordinates|23, 65, -134]], next to [[Dusk]].
## Usage
The Runic Pedestal allows the [[Player]] to fuse [[Runes]], add a [[Runes|Rune]] to an item, or remove a [[Runes|Rune]] from an item. The latter can be done by clicking the [[Cauldron]] and placing the desired item in the slot. The Rune will be destroyed when removing it from an Item.
## Trivia
- There was a glitch where the [[Player]] could get an [[End Portal Frame]] by shift-clicking the "Combine Runes" button. This was later patched at an unknown date.
## History
[[SkyBlock Prototype]]:
|Aug 2, 2019|0.7|Added Runic Pedestal.|
|Nov 2, 2021|Runes are now able to be removed from items.|
|Jul 12, 2023|0.19.1|Fixed [[Runecrafting]] XP not being awarded correctly from combining runes.|

# Sack of Sacks
Aliases: Giant Sack of Sacks, Greater Sack of Sacks, Large Sack of Sacks, Massive Sack of Sacks, Medium Sack of Sacks, Sack Of Sacks, Small Sack of Sacks
Categories: Pages with UIs, Upgrades, Sacks, Tropical Fish Collection, Mechanics, SkyBlock Menu
[Sack of Sacks]
Type: Upgrade
Appearance: Pouch
Collection: Tropical Fish IV (4) ([[Tropical Fish]])
Usage: Store Sacks
[Capacity]
Small: 8 Slots
Medium: 12 Slots
Large: 16 Slots
Greater: 20 Slots
Giant: 24 Slots
Massive: 29 Slots
The Sack of Sacks is a [[Bags|Bag]] that can store [[Sacks]]. It's unlocked at [[Tropical Fish|Tropical Fish IV (4)]] and can be upgraded through the [[Tropical Fish]] collection and [[Account & Profile Upgrades]]: it is accessed from the [[SkyBlock Menu]].
## Usage
The Sack of Sacks allows players to store different [[Sacks]]. Extra slots can be obtained through [[Tropical Fish]] [[Collections|Collection]] and [[Account & Profile Upgrades]].
## Upgrading
The Sack of Sacks is unlocked at [[Tropical Fish|Tropical Fish IV (4)]] and can be upgraded through multiple ways:
### Tropical Fish Collection
|Collection|Total Slots|
|-|-|
|Tropical Fish IV (4) ([[Tropical Fish]])|8|
|Tropical Fish V (5) ([[Tropical Fish]])|12|
|Tropical Fish VI (6) ([[Tropical Fish]])|16|
|Tropical Fish VII (7) ([[Tropical Fish]])|20|
|Tropical Fish VIII (8) ([[Tropical Fish]])|24|
|Tropical Fish IX (9) ([[Tropical Fish]])|29|
### Account and Profile Upgrades
The Sack of Sacks can be increased through [[Account & Profile Upgrades]]. Each upgrade adds additional 2 slots to the Sack of Sacks. The costs and upgrade times are following:
|Tier|Total New Slots|Cost|Time|
|-|-|-|-|
|I|2|Free|4 days|
|II|4|Free|6 days|
|III|6|100 [[SkyBlock Gems]]|12 days|
|IV|8|100 [[SkyBlock Gems]]|12 days|
|V|10|200 [[SkyBlock Gems]]|12 days|
|VI|12|200 [[SkyBlock Gems]]|12 days|
|VII|14|300 [[SkyBlock Gems]]|12 days|
|VIII|16|300 [[SkyBlock Gems]]|12 days|
## Trivia
- Multiple sacks of the same type can be placed into the Sack of Sacks.
  - However, different sacks that can store the same item do not merge their storages. For example having both [[Lava Fishing Sack|Large Lava Fishing Sack]] and [[Mining Sack|Large Mining Sack]] will leave the player with the regular 20,160 base storage for [[Coal]].
## History

|Aug 1, 2022|Added Insert inventory button.|
|Mar 28, 2023|0.18.2|Added two more size tiers.|

# Salesman
Aliases: Bunny Man, Gingerbread Man, Spooky Man, Summer Man, Summer Sloth
Categories: Pages with UIs, Non-player characters
[Salesman]
Type: [[NPC]]
Description: Advertises the Hypixel store.
Location: [[Village]]
Coordinates: -9.5 71 -15.5
The Salesman is an [[NPC]] located in the [[Village]]. During various seasonal events on Hypixel, Salesman will get replaced with NPCs that will award the player [[Reward Bundles]] with festive cosmetics.
## Seasonal Appearance, Interface
Salesman without a Seasonal Appearance only has dialogue when clicked on. Salesman with a Seasonal Appearance opens a [[Reward Bundles]] interface when clicked on.
The Bunny Man NPC
During April to May, the Seasonal Appearance of Salesman is [[Salesman|Bunny Man]]. From Bunny Man, ranked players can redeem various rewards. A player's Hypixel Rank determines the tier of rewards received. The items are purely cosmetic.
The Summer Sloth NPC
During June to August, the Seasonal Appearance of Salesman is [[Salesman|Summer Sloth]]. From Summer Sloth, ranked players can redeem various rewards. A player's Hypixel Rank determines the tier of rewards received. The items are purely cosmetic.
The Spooky Man NPC
During October, the Seasonal Appearance of Salesman is [[Salesman|Spooky Man]]. From Spooky Man, ranked players can redeem various rewards. A player's Hypixel Rank determines the tier of rewards received. The items are purely cosmetic.
The Gingerbread Man NPC
During December, the Seasonal Appearance of Salesman is [[Salesman|Gingerbread Man]]. From Gingerbread Man, ranked players can redeem various rewards. A player's Hypixel Rank determines the tier of rewards received. The items are purely cosmetic.
## Dialogue
Salesman Appearance:
|Condition|Dialogue|
|-|-|
|None|[NPC] Salesman: Thank you for supporting the server and allowing us to maintain SkyBlock! [NPC] Salesman: If you want to support us, head over to the Hypixel Store at store.hypixel.net|
[Seasonal Appearance]
|Condition|Dialogue|
|Claiming a Bundle|You claimed the [<rank>] Bundle! Yay!|
|Already Claimed Bundle|Already claimed!|
|Claiming a Bundle that Requires Higher Rank|Requires [<rank>]|
## Gallery
- Bunny Man
- Summer Sloth
- Summer Man
- Spooky Man
- Gingerbread Man
## History

|Jan 30, 2026|Moved Salesman from 7, 70, -85 ([[Coordinates]]) to -9.5, 71, -15.5 ([[Coordinates]]), due to the redesign of the [[Hub Island]].|
[SkyBlock Prototype]
|Aug 2, 2019|0.7|Added Salesman.|
|June 2020|Changed the Seasonal Appearance in Summer from Summer Man to Summer Sloth.|
|Feb 24, 2026|0.24.2|Moved Salesman from 7, 70, -85 ([[Coordinates]]) to -9.5, 71, -15.5 ([[Coordinates]]), due to the redesign of the [[Hub Island]].|

# Settings
Aliases: Tablist Widgets
Categories: Pages with UIs, SkyBlock Menu, Mechanics
[Settings]
Settings is a menu allowing you to toggle various SkyBlock-related functionality in-game. It is not to be confused with Settings & Visibility which handles Hypixel server related settings and is not specific to SkyBlock.
## Accessing
The Settings menu can either be opened by clicking the Redstone Torch in [[SkyBlock Menu]] or by using the /viewsettings [[Commands|Chat Command]].
## Interface
Tip: click on certain slots to change the UI or go to another article! You may have visited: [[SkyBlock Menu/UI]]
## Settings
Currently, there are 8 available options in the main Settings Menu, some of those have more options in them.
|Name|Icon|Description|Number of Options|Default Toggle|Additional Information|
|-|-|-|-|-|-|
|Personal Settings|Player Head|General settings related to your experience.|7|||
|Comms Settings|[[Sign]]|Tweak notifications and invites you get from other players.|8|||
|Island Settings|[[Repeater]]|Edit SkyBlock settings regarding your island.|4||Can only be accessed in [[Private Island]] and [[The Garden]]|
|API Settings|[[Comparator]]|Edit settings regarding third-party access to your SkyBlock profile.; The Hypixel API lets apps and websites display your player info and games progress.|5||API settings only apply to the current profile|
|Double Tap to Drop|[[Diamond Sword]]|Double tap the drop button to drop certain items.||✔||
|Profile Viewer|Player Head|View player's profiles on right-click.||✔||
|Island Management|Grass Block ([[Grass]])|View and manage island settings.|6|||
|Tablist Widgets|[[Bookshelf]]|View and manage Tablist Widget settings.|1||There is an option for each location in SkyBlock.|
## Personal Settings

|-|-|-|-|
|Sounds|[[Jukebox]]|Edit details about music and sounds that you hear.||
|User Interface|[[Glass]]|Tweak how information is displayed to you.||

# Ship
Aliases: Crew, Crew Member, Crew Members, Fishing Island, Fishing Islands, Golden Pearl, Golden Pearls, Pearl of the Lotus, Pearl of the Swamp, Ship Crew, Ship Navigator, Ship Part, Ship Parts
Categories: Pages with UIs, More info needed, Fishing
[Ship]
The Ship or Ship Navigator is a feature unlocked at [[Fishing|Fishing V (5)]] that allows the player to travel to the [[Backwater Bayou]] and [[Lotus Atoll]].
## Accessing
The Ship can be first accessed in the [[Fishing Outpost]] by speaking to [[Captain Baha]], after unlocking [[Fishing|Fishing V (5)]]. He will task the player with fishing up and bringing him the [[Rusty Ship Engine]], which can then be applied to the Ship Navigator to unlock it.
The ship can then be seen on other [[Fishing]] islands, including the [[Backwater Bayou]] and [[Lotus Atoll]].
## Usage
### Ship Navigator
The front of the Ship features a SHIP NAVIGATOR hologram. Clicking it opens a menu that allows the players to travel to certain [[Fishing]]-related islands.
Each of these islands have their own requirements, [[Sea Creatures]], and [[Chapters]] associated with them. Completing all the chapters for a given island will unlock its Golden Pearl.
|Slot|Fishing Island|Unlock Requirement|Island Features|
|-|-|-|-|
|[[Backwater Bayou]]|[[Backwater Bayou]]|Fishing V (5) ([[Fishing]]); Bringing the [[Rusty Ship Engine]] to [[Captain Baha]]|Fish up Junk ([[Junk Sinker]]) and trade it with [[Junker Joel]] for useful items!; Apply [[Rod Parts]] with [[Roddy]].; Fish Bayou Sea Creatures ([[Sea Creatures/List]]).; Learn about [[Fishing Hotspots]] from [[Hattie]].|
|[[Lotus Atoll]]|[[Lotus Atoll]]|Fishing X (10) ([[Fishing]]); Speaking to [[Captain Baha]] and then [[Researcher Gilbert]] to add them to the Ship Crew|Catch the 12 species of Trophy Frog ([[Trophy Frogs]])!; Fish Lotus Sea Creatures ([[Sea Creatures]]).; Progress through the Lotus Collection ([[Lotus]]).; Make Lily Pads explode ([[Lotus Atoll]]).|
||[[Desecrated Shrine]] [Coming Soon] (This item or feature is 'Coming Soon', which means it is not yet released.)||[[Guardian Slayer]] [Coming Soon]|
### Ship Crew
The Ship Crew is a feature unlocked at [[Fishing|Fishing X (10)]] that allows the player to persuade [[NPC|NPCs]] to join the ship. Each crewmate grants a unique reward, which can include access to new [[Fishing]] islands, and can be seen visibly on the ship.
Despite the unlock requirement, [[Captain Baha]] is always visibly part of the crew, although his [[Fishing Wisdom]] buff does still require [[Fishing|Fishing X (10)]].
|Slot|NPC|Perk|
|-|-|-|
|[[Captain Baha]]|[[Captain Baha]]|+0.5 [[Fishing Wisdom]]|
|[[Researcher Gilbert]]|[[Researcher Gilbert]]|Access to the [[Lotus Atoll]].|
### Ship Parts
Certain items can be applied to the Ship to grant permanent [[Stats]] and [[SkyBlock Levels|SkyBlock XP]]:
|Slot|Item|Reward|Obtaining|
|-|-|-|-|
|[[Bronze Ship Helm]]|[[Bronze Ship Helm]]|+0.5 [[Fishing Wisdom]]; +5 SkyBlock XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|Crafted using 64 (1 stack) [[Dark Oak Log]] and 4 [[Bronze Bowl]].|
|[[Bronze Ship Engine]]|[[Bronze Ship Engine]]|+0.5 [[Fishing Wisdom]]; +5 SkyBlock XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|Purchased from [[Junker Joel]] for 64 [[Rusty Coin]] and 8 [[Busted Belt Buckle]].|
|[[Bronze Ship Hull]]|[[Bronze Ship Hull]]|+0.5 [[Fishing Wisdom]]; +5 SkyBlock XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|Crafted using 3 [[Torn Cloth]] and 3 [[Bronze Bowl]].|
### Golden Pearls
Golden Pearls are rewards that can be earned on each [[Fishing]] island after completing its final [[Chapters|Chapter]]. Each pearl grants a special perk.
|Slot|Pearl|Island|Rewards|
|-|-|-|-|
|Pearl of the Swamp|Pearl of the Swamp|[[Backwater Bayou]]|+1 [[Treasure Chance]]|
|Pearl of the Lotus|Pearl of the Lotus|[[Lotus Atoll]]|+2.5 [[Trophy Chance]]|
### Renaming
The Ship has various default names, but can be renamed. Names can only contain letters or numbers.
Expand
Kraken's Smile Sea Dragon Steadfast Storyteller The Aurora The Blight Giant The Maelstrom The Merchant The Sea Serpent The Sky Swan The Wind Seeker Wanderer [More Info Needed] (More infomation needed; When this info is added, remove this.)
## Game Messages
|Message|Condition|
|-|-|
|You equipped your Ship with a Rusty Ship Engine!|Applying the [[Rusty Ship Engine]]|
|Your Ship is now named <name>!|Renaming the Ship|
|The name can only contain letters or numbers!|Trying to rename the Ship to an invalid name|
|Your Ship's name cannot be longer than 16 characters!|Trying to rename the Ship with too many characters|
|That name is inappropriate! Choose another.|Trying to rename the Ship to an inappropriate name|
|Invalid name!|Trying to rename without providing a name or renaming to "null"|
## Trivia
- Despite not being a confirmed [[Ship|Fishing Island]], an island named World's End is implied to be the final Fishing Island unlocked as per [[Captain Baha]]'s dialogue.
- The ship cannot be renamed to "null" (not case sensitive).

# Skills
Aliases: Skill, Skill Exp, Skill XP
Categories: Pages with UIs, Mechanics, SkyBlock Menu, Skills, Combat, Mining, Fishing, Alchemy, Foraging, Runecrafting, Dungeons, Carpentry
The skills menu
There are thirteen Skills in Hypixel SkyBlock, which are automatically leveled up based on the actions performed by a player.
In order to gain XP for a specific Skill, players need to perform tasks corresponding to a Skill. For example, to level up [[Mining]], the player would need to mine blocks. Players can also level up their Skills by collecting resources from their [[Minions]] for a reduced amount of XP.
Increasing the [[Wisdom]] of a specific Skill will increase how much XP is gained for that Skill. An example of how this can be done is by equipping a [[Rabbit Pet]] to grant [[Farming Wisdom]].
Leveling up Skills provides rewards and also permanently increases various player [[Stats]], as well as [[SkyBlock Levels|SkyBlock XP]]. For example, leveling up [[Farming]] gives the player coins and increases their [[Health]].
The player's Skill level can be seen by opening the [[SkyBlock Menu]] and going to "Skills" (displayed as a [[Diamond Sword]]). This also displays average Skill level, across all skills except [[Social]], [[Runecrafting]] and [[Dungeoneering]].
## Interface
Tip: click on certain slots to change the UI or go to another article! You may have visited: [[SkyBlock Menu/UI]]

|[[Farming]]|[[Health]] Bonus [[Farming Fortune]] Bonus|Collecting naturally grown crops such as [[Wheat]], Carrots ([[Carrot]]), Potatoes ([[Potato]]), Pumpkins ([[Pumpkin]]) and Melons ([[Melon]]) Collecting from [[Farming]] [[Minions]] such as Wheat Minions ([[Wheat Minion]]), Carrot Minions ([[Carrot Minion]]), Potato Minions ([[Potato Minion]]), Pumpkin Minions ([[Pumpkin Minion]]),Nether Warts ([[Nether Wart]]) and Melon Minions ([[Melon Minion]]) Shearing [[Sheep]]|LX (60)|
|[[Fishing]]|[[Health]] Bonus [[Treasure Chance]] Unlocks new [[Sea Creatures]] (for specific levels)|Fishing up loot with any Fishing Rod ([[Fishing Rods]]) Killing [[Sea Creatures]] that can be caught through fishing Collecting from [[Fishing]] [[Minions]] such as Fishing Minions ([[Fishing Minion]]) and Clay Minions ([[Clay Minion]])|L(50)|
|[[Mining]]|[[Defense]] Bonus [[Mining Fortune]] Unlocks [[Reforging]] with [[Reforge Stones]] (for specific levels)|Mining naturally generated [[Ores]] and Stones such as [[Cobblestone]], Iron Ore ([[Ores]]), Gold Ore ([[Ores]]), Coal Ore ([[Ores]]) , Lapis Lazuli Ore ([[Ores]]), Diamond Ore ([[Ores]]) and so on. Collecting from [[Mining]] [[Minions]], such as Cobblestone Minions ([[Cobblestone Minion]]), Iron Minions ([[Iron Minion]]), Gold Minions ([[Gold Minion]]), Coal Minions ([[Coal Minion]]), Lapis Minions ([[Lapis Minion]]), and Snow Minions ([[Snow Minion]]) [[Commissions]] and Commission Milestones|LX (60)|
|[[Foraging]]|[[Strength]] Bonus [[Foraging Fortune]] Bonus|Chopping down naturally growing trees and collecting logs such as [[Oak Log]], [[Birch Log]], [[Jungle Log]] and [[Dark Oak Log]] Picking naturally grown [[Flowers]] such as Dandelions ([[Flowers]]) and Poppies ([[Flowers]]), or galatea resources such as [[Lushlilac]] or [[Sea Lumies]]. Collecting from [[Foraging]] [[Minions]] such as Oak Minions ([[Oak Minion]]), Birch Minions ([[Birch Minion]]), Jungle Minions ([[Jungle Minion]]), Dark Oak Minions ([[Dark Oak Minion]]) and Flower Minions ([[Flower Minion]]) Obtained from [[Tree Gifts]] and Agatha's Contest ([[Starlyn Contest]]) rewards.|LVII (57)|
|[[Enchanting]]|[[Intelligence]] Bonus [[Ability Damage]] Bonus Increases XP orbs gained from any source Unlocking [[Enchantments]]|Combining items in an [[Anvil]] (i.e. adding books to armor; combining books does not give xp) Using an [[Enchantment Table]] Conducting Experiments and playing minigames with an [[Experimentation Table]] Fishing up Experience Bottles ([[Experience Bottle]]), Grand Experience Bottles ([[Grand Experience Bottle]]) or Titanic Experience Bottles ([[Titanic Experience Bottle]]).|LX (60)|
|[[Alchemy]]|[[Intelligence]] Bonus Increases duration for brewed [[Potions]]|Brewing [[Potions]] Collecting from [[Alchemy]] [[Minions]] such as Nether Wart Minions ([[Nether Wart Minion]])|L (50)|
|[[Carpentry]]|[[Health]] Bonus Unlock [[Furniture]] Recipes|Crafting items|L (50)|
|[[Taming]]|[[Pet Luck]] Bonus Increases Pet XP gain|Earning Pet ([[Pets]]) XP|LX (60)|
|[[Hunting]]|[[Hunting Fortune]] Bonus Increases chance to Charm the mobs you defeat and capture their Shard|Hunting mobs Fusing Shards Trap and Salt Hunt Milestones Rewards from Hunting related Collections, such as [[Tender Wood]], [[Vinesap]] and [[Lushlilac]]|L (50)|
|[[Dungeoneering]]|[[Health]] Bonus Increases stats while in [[Dungeons]]|Participating in [[Dungeons]]|L (50)|
[Cosmetic Skills]
|[[Runecrafting]]|Access higher level [[Runes]]|Killing Runic Mobs ([[Runecrafting]]) or Bosses such as Ender Dragons ([[Ender Dragon]]) Attempting to combine Runes in the Rune Pedestal ([[Dusk]])|XXV (25)|
|[[Social]]|Access more social games and coins to spend at Amelia's Shop ([[Amelia]])|Having unique players visit the player's island Visiting other player's islands|XXV (25)|
[Runecrafting levels up differently depending on the player's Rank (Ranks). Unranked players can only reach level 3. 1Default (Ranks): x1.0 XP 2[VIP] (Ranks): x1.1 XP 3[VIP+] (Ranks): x1.25 XP 4[MVP] (Ranks): x1.5 XP 5[MVP+] (Ranks): x2 XP 6[MVP++] (Ranks): x3 XP Cosmetic Skills do not affect skill average.]

## Maxed Out Stats
These are the bonus stats a player would receive when having the maximum level on all Skills.
- [[SkyBlock Levels|+8120 XP]], yielding 81 SkyBlock Levels and 20 SkyBlock Experience
- +534 [[Health]] (+242 from [[Farming]], +192 from [[Fishing]] and +100 from [[Dungeoneering]] - The Catacombs 1-50)
- +106 [[Defense]] (from [[Mining]])
- +30% [[Crit Chance]] (from [[Combat]])
- +100 [[Strength]] (from [[Foraging]])
- +192 [[Intelligence]] (+106 from [[Enchanting]] and +86 from [[Alchemy]])
- +30 [[Ability Damage]] (from [[Enchanting]])
- +60 [[Pet Luck]] (from [[Taming]])
- +240 [[Farming Fortune]]: 100% Chance of Triple Drops, and 40% Chance of Quadruple Drops from Crops (from [[Farming]])
- +240 [[Mining Fortune]]: 100% Chance of Triple Drops, and 40% Chance of Quadruple Drops from Ores (from [[Mining]])
- +50 [[Hunting Fortune]]: 50% Chance to Double the amount of shards a player receives (from [[Hunting]])
- +210% Damage dealt to mobs (from [[Combat]])
- +228 [[Foraging Fortune]]: 100% Chance of Triple Drops, and 28% Chance of Quadruple Drops from Logs (from [[Foraging]])
- +5 [[Treasure Chance]]: +5% Chance of catching treasure while fishing (from [[Fishing]])
- +300% Experience gained from any source (from [[Enchanting]])
- +50% Duration of potions, +12h duration from [[God Potion|God Potions]], +36h duration from [[Mixins]] (from [[Alchemy]])
- +60% Extra pet XP (from [[Taming]])
- +485% Stats of your dungeon items while in The Catacombs (from [[Dungeoneering]] - The Catacombs 1-50. 50+ Does not provide any additional stat boost)
- +2% [[Charm Chance]] (from [[Hunting]])
## Skill XP Boost Potions
When these [[Potions]] are splashed or consumed, they allow the player to get [[Wisdom]], which grants an increase in Skill Experience. Depending on its level, they can last for 12, 24, or 36 minutes. [[Pets]] are also affected because they constantly gain a quarter of any Skill XP.

|[[Potions]]|Mining XP Boost|[[Gifts]]|Gain [[Mining Wisdom]]|+5 [[Mining Wisdom]]|+10 [[Mining Wisdom]]|+20 [[Mining Wisdom]]|
|[[Potions]]|Combat XP Boost|[[Gifts]]|Gain [[Combat Wisdom]]|+5 [[Combat Wisdom]]|+10 [[Combat Wisdom]]|+20 [[Combat Wisdom]]|

## Trivia
- Once the player maxes out a skill, the skill counter when the player earns XP for that skill will show that the next level requires 0 XP.
  - E.g. Earning +1 XP will display "+1/0
- Players don't get XP from killing mobs on their [[Private Island]].
- A 20% Skill XP boost, together with a +10 [[Magic Find]] boost, was active between March 2020 and October 20, 2020.
  - The boost was for the COVID-19 pandemic. It was originally intended to last for only a short time.
- The Social skill was leaked by 52[[Ranks|[HELPER]]] Caameroon at around 1:50 PM EDT on Nov. 9th, 2020.
- The player "Linman" was the first to achieve all Level 50 skills and thus was also the first player to achieve 50 Skill Average
- From June 23rd to July 2nd, 2021, during SkyBlock Year 144-146, a 2x Skill XP was active, due to Hypixel being in maintenance mode a few days before.
  - The rest of the games had 3x experience, but SkyBlock didn't follow that due to fears that it would unbalance the game.

# Taming
Categories: Pages with UIs, Potions, Skills, Pets
Taming is one of the [[Skills]] available for players to level up as they perform certain actions that give them Taming XP. Leveling Taming increases the player's [[Pets|Extra Pet XP]], [[Pets|Exp Share rate]], and [[Pet Luck]].
Like other Skills, [[Roman Numerals]] are used to express its levels in-game.
Although Taming goes from levels 1 - 60 (I - LX), there is 50 XP required to reach the first level, meaning there is a "level 0", just like all other skills.
The Taming Level Cap can be increased through [[George]], to a maximum of Level 60 (LX). In order to reach this cap, players have to give George specific [[Pets]] (with the same or higher rarity):
- [[Rarity|EPIC]] [[Rift Ferret Pet]]
- [[Rarity|EPIC]] [[Slug Pet]]
- [[Rarity|EPIC]] [[Spirit Pet]]
- [[Rarity|EPIC]] [[Giraffe Pet]]
- [[Rarity|EPIC]] [[Jellyfish Pet]]
- [[Rarity|EPIC]] [[Bal Pet]]
- [[Rarity|EPIC]] [[Baby Yeti Pet]]
- [[Rarity|LEGENDARY]] [[Black Cat Pet]]
- [[Rarity|RARE]] [[Wisp Pet|Frost Wisp Pet]]
- [[Rarity|MYTHIC]] [[Enderman Pet]]
The Pets will not be given back to you, so it's recommended to give [Lv1] pets to [[George]] (or whatever is cheapest).
## Leveling XP
The amount of Taming XP gained depends on the Skill XP gained and on the Pet's type. Using a Pet whose type differs from the skill XP gained results in less Taming XP earned. A pet must be active to earn Taming XP. Different skills result in different amounts of Taming XP gained.
## Zoologist
Zoologist is an ability exclusively unlocked by leveling up the Taming Skill. Each level of Zoologist grants +1% Pet XP gained and +1 [[Pet Luck]].
Each level of Taming grants one level of Zoologist. For example, if the player were on Taming level 15, the player would therefore also be on Zoologist level 15.
## Leveling Rewards
The cumulative rewards at Taming level XXV (25) are:
- +25% extra Pet XP
- +25 [[Pet Luck]]
- 1,334,100 [[Coins]]
- [[SkyBlock Levels|200 SkyBlock XP]]
The cumulative rewards at Taming level L (50) are:
- +50% extra Pet XP
- +50 [[Pet Luck]]
- 17,259,100 [[Coins]]
- [[SkyBlock Levels|700 SkyBlock XP]]
The cumulative rewards at Taming Level LX (60)
- +60% extra Pet XP
- +60 [[Pet Luck]]
- 27,259,100 [[Coins]]
- [[SkyBlock Levels|1,000 SkyBlock XP]]
## Uses
Leveling up Taming is not only good for extra pet luck but can also be used in order to upgrade pets using Kat. Players will need Taming Level 10 to get a pet from [[Rarity|RARE]] to [[Rarity|EPIC]], Taming Level 20 in order to get a pet from [[Rarity|EPIC]] to [[Rarity|LEGENDARY]], and Taming Level 25 to get a pet from [[Rarity|LEGENDARY]] to [[Rarity|MYTHIC]]. It's also very effective for leveling pets faster if the player has a decent level of at least 25. It can also be used to upgrade the base [[Damage]] of the [[Daedalus Blade]].
## Trivia
- Doing an action with a pet that is already level 100 still yields Taming XP. The excess of XP will be "credited" to the pet, so if its rarity is upgraded, the excess will be added to the pet's XP. This means that the player's pet can still be level 100 after picking it up from Kat, if it has enough excessive XP at the previous rarity. However, you cannot feed a level 100 pet [[Pet Candies|Pet Food]].
- All Pet XP boosts work multiplicatively together: [[Beastmaster Crest]], [[Diana]] and [[Pet Items]].
- It costs , 3,000 [[Motes]], and 12 [[Riftwart Roots]] to acquire Taming 60.

# Tank
Categories: Pages with UIs, Dungeons, Classes
The Tank Dungeon Orb
Tank is a dungeon [[Classes|class]] that specializes in [[Effective Health]] and being able to take large amount of damage and keeping [[Mob|mobs]] off their teammates.
## Class Passives
- Base +100 [[Health]], +50 [[Defense]], and +10 [[Vitality]]
- Protective Barrier: Gain permanent +25% (Multiplicative) [[Defense]]. Upon falling below 50% [[Health]], gain an [[Absorption]] Shield 10% of your [[Health]]. Cooldown: 90s
- Taunt: Increases the chance for mobs to target you when you are above 25% [[Health|HP]].
- Diversion: Divert 80% of the damage taken by your teammates within 30 blocks around you.
- Defensive Stance: Grants immunity to knockback from mobs.
## Dungeon Orb Abilities
- Seismic Wave: A Seismic Wave emerges from underneath you and travels in a straight line. Deals 20000 + 10% every 50 [[Defense]] in damage to any enemy in its path. Cooldown: 15s
- Castle of Stone: Fortifies your [[Defense]] by giving a 70% damage reduction for 20 seconds. Also aggros all enemies in a 10 block radius. Cooldown: 150s
## Ghost Abilities
- Stun Potion: Throw a potion which temporarily stuns all monsters in a 10 block radius.
- Absorption Potion: Throw a potion which gives all teammates in a 10 block radius +200 [[Absorption]] for 3 seconds.
## Leveling Rewards
You can level up the tank class while playing as a tank or if you have a teammate that is tank, you get some tank XP as a team bonus.
For every tank level up to level 49 you get:
- +1 [[Defense]]
- +0.1 [[Vitality]]
- 0.2% [[Defense]]
- + 20000-50000 Seismic Wave Damage
- +10 [[Absorption]] from Ghost Absorption Potion
At Tank Level 50, you instead gain +2 [[Defense]], +0.2 [[Vitality]], along with +0.2% [[Defense]], +10 [[Absorption]] from Ghost [[Absorption]] Potion, and +100000 Seismic Wave Damage
At Tank Level 50, you receive the following total buffs:
- +51 [[Defense]], for a total of +101 [[Defense]]
- +5.1 [[Vitality]], for a total of +15.1 [[Vitality]]
- +10% [[Defense]] for a total of permanent +35% [[Defense]] (Multiplicative)
- +500 [[Absorption]] from Ghost [[Absorption]] Potion, for a total of 700 [[Absorption]]
When you are the only tank, the buffs change as following:
- +25% -> +50% (Multiplicative) permanent [[Defense]]
- Upon Falling below 50% -> 100% [[Health]], gain an [[Absorption]] Shield 10% -> 20% of your [[Health]]. Note: As the health at which this activates gets increased to 100%, it will always trigger every 90s -> 45s. It is unknown if this is intended or not.
## Milestones
Tank class can reach its milestones by tanking and dealing damage in the dungeon. Refer [[Class Milestones|here]] for a table showcasing class milestones.
## Trivia
- The Castle of Stone perk is a reference to the guardian skill Castle of Stone from Log Horizon, of which grants one immunity to damage for a short duration. The perk itself does more resemble the guardian skill Anchor Howl, of which aggros enemies and grants one increased defense for a short duration.
- There is a glitch where, when changing your armor set via the Wardrobe, the Tank [[Knockback]] immunity perk stops working.
- There is another glitch where, if mobs are standing on carpet, the mobs on carpet will not take any damage from the Seismic Wave ability. Noticeable when you are soloing the [[The Catacombs - Floor VI|Floor VI]] boss fight.

# The Hex
Aliases: Hex, Modifier, Modifiers
Categories: Pages with UIs
[The Hex]
The Hex is a feature that allows players to examine their items and to see how they can upgrade them further. It is located in the [[Hexatorum]] in the [[Hub Island|Hub]].
## Mechanics
The Hex allows players to insert an item into its menu and examine it. It shows all the upgrades the item currently has and how much further it can be upgraded. It can apply all listed upgrades to the item. To upgrade gear, it can take items from the player's inventory, and [[Storage]]. It can also use the player's [[Coins]] to buy upgrades from the [[Bazaar]].
The Hex can be used to remove enchantments, including [[Stacking Enchantments]], [[Endcaps|Endcap Upgrades]], and those applied using [[Enchanted Book|Enchanted Books]] rather than the [[Enchantment Table]].
The Hex requires [[Museum|Museum Milestone 8]] to be used.

# Time Pocket
Aliases: Aging Item, Aging Items, Time Bag, Time Pocket Bag, Timebag, Timepocket
Categories: Pages with UIs, Timite Collection, Aging Items, Upgrades, Collections
[Time Pocket]
Type: Upgrade
Appearance: Melted Clock
Collection: Timite II (2) ([[Timite]])
Usage: Stores Evolving Items
[Capacity]
Default: 6 slots
Large: 9 slots
Upgrade 2: 12 slots
The Time Pocket is a [[Bags|Bag]] that appears in the Your Bags menu once the player has reached [[Timite|Timite II (2)]]. It can also be accessed with the /timebag, /timep, /timepocket or /tb commands if a [[Booster Cookie]] is active. It is used to store [[Time Pocket|Aging Items]], making them age twice as fast.
## Obtaining
The Time Pocket is automatically unlocked once the player reaches [[Timite|Timite II (2)]].
### Upgrading
The Time Pocket can be upgraded from 6 to 9 slots for 1 [[Discrite]]. It can then be further upgraded from 9 to 12 slots for 1 [[Discrite|Spotlite]].
## Usage
The Time Pocket stores items that can evolve over time, called Aging Items. The following items can be stored:
- [[Bottle of Jyrre]] and [[Bottle of Jyrre|Refined Bottle of Jyrre]]
- [[Dark Cacao Truffle]] and [[Dark Cacao Truffle|Refined Dark Cacao Truffle]]
- [[Discrite]] and [[Discrite|Spotlite]]
- [[Moby-Duck]] and [[Moby-Duck|Moby-Duck: Collector's Edition]]
- [[Rosewater Flask]] and [[Rosewater Flask|Filled Rosewater Flask]]
- [[Not Very Moldy Bread]] and [[Not Very Moldy Bread|Very Moldy Bread]]
Items in the Time Pocket evolve twice as fast, meaning an item that normally takes 300 hours to evolve only takes 150 hours. Completing the [[Catacombs Puzzle Rooms|Quiz]] puzzle in Dungeons will also add 2 hours instead of 1 to each item in the Time Pocket (with the exceptions of [[Rosewater Flask|Rosewater Flasks]], which are unaffected by the puzzle). The effect of [[Enchanted Clock]] is also doubled.
## Trivia
- The Time Pocket head sprite might be a reference to a painting named The Persistence of Memory by Salvador Dalí.
## History

# Tokens
Categories: Pages with UIs, Confirmations needed, Currencies
Tokens are a [[Currency]] used when fighting [[Kuudra]], they can be used in [[Kuudra|Kuudra Perk Shop]] to purchase various perks that last for this one fight. Tokens are obtained by killing mobs during Kuudra or hitting Kuudra's Tentacles with cannons. They are awarded to all players equally.

# Trades
Aliases: Trade
Categories: Pages with UIs, Mechanics, SkyBlock Menu, Items
The Trades Menu is located next to the close button at the bottom of the Recipe Book menu.
  Not to be confused with [[Trading]], a game mechanic where you can trade items with other players.
  For [[Stranded]] Villager Trades, see [[Stranded/Trades]].
Trades are a SkyBlock Mechanic where players can trade items in their inventory for other items supplied by the server, using a submenu located in the [[Recipe Book]] in their [[SkyBlock Menu]]. Trades can only be unlocked through a [[Collections|Collection]].
Trading for resources does not affect collection progress.
Players can directly sell items from the Trade Menu if they have a [[Booster Cookie]] active.
## Accessing & Using
The Trades menu can be accessed by opening the [[Recipe Book]] and then by clicking on the Emerald next to the close button on the menu, labeled "Trades."
A new menu will open, with a list of Trades. Slots filled with Grey Dye are currently locked, meaning that the player needs to unlock them, likely through a [[Collections|Collection]] unlock. The player can hover over unlocked items to the item's description and the required items or currency ded to trade for the item. Finally, clicking on an item will trade for the item if you have the required materials, or play a sound effect and throw an error if not. There is a daily buy limit of 640 on all items.
The arrow at the bottom-middle of the screen will go back to the [[SkyBlock Menu]], and the barrier will close the menu outright and go back to gameplay.

## Trivia
- The whole seed collection unlocks trades, which are shown in the diagram above.
- The daily buy limit of 640 is increased to 6400 while [[Diaz]] is mayor.
